629:     without_distribution: bool = False,
630:     unassigned_packed_order_ids: frozenset[uuid.UUID] = frozenset(),
631:     discrepancies: tuple[composition_svc.SupplyCompositionDiscrepancy, ...] = (),
632:     source_plan: source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan | None = None,
633: ) -> list[DeliveryCheck]:
634:     # «Сдать без Честного знака» снимает НАШЕ требование маркировки по поставке.
635:     # Требование самого Wildberries, записанное в required_meta_json заказа, этим
636:     # флагом не отменяется: такой заказ по-прежнему не уедет, и это правильно.
637:     honest_sign_skipped = supply.honest_sign_skipped_at is not None
638:     # Складской прогресс остаётся предупреждением для обеих площадок.
639:     # Требование Ozon собрать отправления проверяется отдельно по ответу /ship.
640:     soft = CHECK_WARNING
641:     checks: list[DeliveryCheck] = []
642: 
643:     if supply.status in _DELIVER_BLOCKED_SUPPLY_STATUSES:
644:         checks.append(
645:             DeliveryCheck(
646:                 code="supply_bad_status",
647:                 message="Поставка уже передана или закрыта.",
648:                 ok=False,
649:                 severity=CHECK_BLOCKER,
650:             )
651:         )
652: 
653:     if not orders:
654:         checks.append(
655:             DeliveryCheck(
656:                 code="supply_empty",
657:                 message="Поставка пуста — нет заказов.",
658:                 ok=False,
659:                 severity=CHECK_BLOCKER,
660:             )
661:         )
662: 
663:     for discrepancy in discrepancies:
664:         missing_from_wb = discrepancy.code == "local_order_not_in_wb"
665:         terminal_wb_order = supply.marketplace == "wb" and discrepancy.code in {
666:             "terminal_order",
667:             "terminal_local_order",
668:         }
669:         ignored = missing_from_wb or terminal_wb_order
670:         checks.append(
671:             DeliveryCheck(
672:                 code=(
673:                     "local_order_not_in_wb_ignored"
674:                     if missing_from_wb
675:                     else (
676:                         "wb_terminal_order_ignored"
677:                         if terminal_wb_order
678:                         else "wb_supply_composition_discrepancy"
679:                     )
680:                 ),
681:                 message=(
682:                     f"Заказ WB {discrepancy.wb_order_id} отсутствует в фактическом составе "
683:                     "и не будет списан."
684:                     if missing_from_wb
685:                     else (
686:                         f"Заказ WB {discrepancy.wb_order_id} уже отменён или закрыт; "
687:                         "он исключён из списания и не блокирует передачу поставки."
688:                         if terminal_wb_order
689:                         else f"Заказ WB {discrepancy.wb_order_id}: {discrepancy.code}."
690:                     )
691:                 ),
692:                 # Keep a visible warning for a terminal WB order, but never make
693:                 # the operator repair marketplace history before dispatching the
694:                 # remaining active orders. Reconciliation already excludes this
695:                 # order from source planning, write-off and local delivery state.
696:                 ok=ignored and not terminal_wb_order,
697:                 severity=(
698:                     CHECK_INFO
699:                     if (ignored and not terminal_wb_order)
700:                     else CHECK_WARNING
701:                     if terminal_wb_order
702:                     else CHECK_BLOCKER
703:                 ),
704:                 order_id=discrepancy.local_order_id,
705:             )
706:         )
707: 
708:     for order in orders:
709:         if order.status in _TERMINAL_ORDER_STATUSES:
710:             checks.append(
711:                 DeliveryCheck(
712:                     code="order_terminal",
713:                     message="Заказ из фактического состава уже отменён или закрыт.",
714:                     ok=False,
715:                     severity=CHECK_BLOCKER,
716:                     order_id=order.id,
717:                 )
718:             )
719: 
720:         if supply.marketplace == "wb":
721:             if (
722:                 order.sticker_status not in _DELIVER_READY_STICKER_STATUSES
723:                 or not order.sticker_file
724:             ):
725:                 checks.append(
726:                     DeliveryCheck(
727:                         code="order_sticker_not_ready",
728:                         message="Стикер заказа WB ещё не подготовлен.",
729:                         ok=False,
730:                         severity=CHECK_WARNING,
731:                         order_id=order.id,
732:                     )
733:                 )
734:             else:
735:                 checks.append(
736:                     DeliveryCheck(
737:                         code="order_sticker_ready",
738:                         message="Стикер заказа WB готов.",
739:                         ok=True,
740:                         severity=CHECK_INFO,
741:                         order_id=order.id,
742:                     )
743:                 )
744: 
745:         if supply.marketplace == "wb" and order.product_id is None:
746:             checks.append(
747:                 DeliveryCheck(
748:                     code="order_product_not_mapped",
749:                     message=(
750:                         "Товар заказа не сопоставлен с карточкой в WMS — "
751:                         "со склада он списан не будет. Передаче не мешает."
752:                     ),
753:                     ok=False,
754:                     severity=CHECK_WARNING,
755:                     order_id=order.id,
756:                 )
757:             )
758: 
759:         product = order.product
760:         if (
761:             product is not None
762:             and product.requires_honest_sign
763:             and not _order_has_sgtin_marking(order)
764:             and not honest_sign_skipped
765:         ):
766:             checks.append(
767:                 DeliveryCheck(
768:                     code="marking_required",
769:                     message=(
770:                         "Честный знак не нанесён. Передаче не мешает, нанести можно и после неё."
771:                     ),
772:                     ok=False,
773:                     severity=soft,
774:                     order_id=order.id,
775:                 )
776:             )
777:         elif not marking_svc.compute_delivery_allowed(order, list(order.markings)):
778:             checks.append(
779:                 DeliveryCheck(
780:                     code="marking_not_allowed",
781:                     message=marking_svc.delivery_marking_message(order, list(order.markings)),
782:                     ok=False,
783:                     severity=soft,
784:                     order_id=order.id,
785:                 )
786:             )
787:         elif order.required_meta_json:
788:             checks.append(
789:                 DeliveryCheck(
790:                     code="marking_allowed",
791:                     message=marking_svc.delivery_marking_message(order, list(order.markings)),
792:                     ok=True,
793:                     severity=CHECK_INFO,
794:                     order_id=order.id,
795:                 )
796:             )
797: 
798:     if supply.delivery_type == FBS_DELIVERY_TYPE_PVZ and has_physical_boxes:
799:         if not supply.trbxes:
800:             checks.append(
801:                 DeliveryCheck(
802:                     code="box_qr_not_ready",
803:                     message=(
804:                         "QR коробов ПВЗ ещё не созданы. "
805:                         "Передаче не мешает, напечатать можно и после неё."
806:                     ),
807:                     ok=False,
808:                     severity=soft,
809:                 )
810:             )
811:         elif not cargo_qr_ready:
812:             checks.append(
813:                 DeliveryCheck(
814:                     code="cargo_place_qr_not_ready",
815:                     message=(
816:                         "QR грузомест не напечатаны. "
817:                         "Передаче не мешает, напечатать можно и после неё."
818:                     ),
819:                     ok=False,
820:                     severity=soft,
821:                 )
822:             )
823:         else:
824:             checks.append(
825:                 DeliveryCheck(
826:                     code="cargo_places_ready",
827:                     message="Грузоместа и QR готовы.",
828:                     ok=True,
829:                     severity=CHECK_INFO,
830:                 )
831:             )
832: 
833:     if boxes_required and not has_physical_boxes:
834:         checks.append(
835:             DeliveryCheck(
836:                 code="physical_boxes_required",
837:                 message="В поставке пока нет коробов.",
838:                 ok=False,
839:                 severity=soft,
840:             )
841:         )
842:     if boxes_required and without_distribution and has_physical_boxes:
843:         checks.append(
844:             DeliveryCheck(
845:                 code="boxes_without_distribution",
846:                 message="Короба созданы без распределения товаров.",
847:                 ok=True,
848:                 severity=CHECK_INFO,
849:             )
850:         )
851:     else:
852:         for order_id in sorted(unassigned_packed_order_ids):
853:             checks.append(
854:                 DeliveryCheck(
855:                     code="packed_order_unassigned",
856:                     message="Для заказа не указан короб.",
857:                     ok=False,
858:                     severity=soft,
859:                     order_id=order_id,
860:                 )
861:             )
862: 
863:     if source_plan is not None:
864:         for resolution in source_plan.resolutions:
865:             if resolution.shortage_quantity:
866:                 checks.append(
867:                     DeliveryCheck(
868:                         code="negative_stock",
869:                         message=(
870:                             f"Не хватает {resolution.shortage_quantity} шт.; после "
871:                             "подтверждения остаток будет списан в минус."
872:                         ),
873:                         ok=False,
874:                         severity=CHECK_WARNING,
875:                         order_id=resolution.fbs_order_id,
876:                     )
877:                 )
878: 
879:     return _apply_wb_blocker_policy(checks, supply.marketplace)
880: 
881: 
882: def _apply_wb_blocker_policy(
883:     checks: list[DeliveryCheck], marketplace: str | None
884: ) -> list[DeliveryCheck]:
885:     """Страховка от повторного появления внутренних блокировок WB.
886: 
887:     Даже если кто-то заведёт новую проверку и по привычке пометит её блокером,
888:     для Wildberries она станет предупреждением — кроме кодов из
889:     WB_ALLOWED_BLOCKER_CODES. Чтобы сделать проверку WB блокирующей, придётся
890:     осознанно дописать её в этот список, а список заморожен тестом.
891:     """
892:     if marketplace != "wb":
893:         return checks
894:     return [
895:         check
896:         if check.severity != CHECK_BLOCKER or check.code in WB_ALLOWED_BLOCKER_CODES
897:         else replace(check, severity=CHECK_WARNING)
898:         for check in checks
899:     ]
900: 
901: 
902: def _checks_to_payload(checks: list[DeliveryCheck]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
903:     return [
904:         {
905:             "code": check.code,
906:             "message": check.message,
907:             "ok": check.ok,
908:             "severity": check.severity,
909:             "order_id": str(check.order_id) if check.order_id is not None else None,
910:         }
911:         for check in checks
912:     ]
913: 
914: 
915: def _validate_checks_pass(checks: list[DeliveryCheck]) -> None:
916:     for check in checks:
917:         if check.severity == CHECK_BLOCKER:
918:             if check.code in {
919:                 "wb_supply_composition_discrepancy",
920:                 "order_terminal",
921:                 "fbs_shipment_source_missing",
922:             }:
923:                 raise FbsShipmentError(
924:                     check.code,
925:                     context={"order_id": str(check.order_id) if check.order_id else None},
926:                     http_status=409,
927:                 )
928:             raise FbsShipmentError(check.code)
929: 
930: 
931: def _checks_allow_delivery(checks: list[DeliveryCheck]) -> bool:
932:     return not any(check.severity == CHECK_BLOCKER for check in checks)
933: 
934: 
935: async def _actual_wb_orders_and_source_plan(
936:     session: AsyncSession,
937:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
938:     supply: FbsSupply,
939:     http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
940:     token: str,
941:     *,
942:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
943: ) -> tuple[
944:     list[FbsOrder],
945:     composition_svc.SupplyCompositionResult,
946:     source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan,
947: ]:
948:     try:
949:         composition = await composition_svc.reconcile_actual_wb_supply_composition(
950:             session,
951:             tenant_id,
952:             supply.id,
953:             http_client=http_client,
954:             api_token=token,
955:         )
956:     except WildberriesClientError as exc:
957:         raise _shipment_error_from_wb(
958:             exc,
959:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
960:             seller_id=supply.seller_id,
961:             local_entity_id=supply.id,
962:             wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
963:             event="fbs shipment WB composition read failed",
964:             retryable=True,
965:             http_status=502,
966:         ) from exc
967:     except composition_svc.FbsSupplyCompositionError as exc:
968:         raise FbsShipmentError(exc.code, http_status=409) from exc
969:     await _sync_supply_orders_from_wb(
970:         session,
971:         tenant_id,
972:         supply,
973:         http_client,
974:         token,
975:         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
976:     )
977:     orders = [
978:         order for order in composition.active_orders if order.status not in _TERMINAL_ORDER_STATUSES
979:     ]
980:     # ⛔ Раньше здесь падало 409 на первом же заказе без сопоставленного товара,
981:     # причём ДО того, как соберётся список проверок. То есть вся защита от
982:     # блокировок проходила мимо: один заказ с незнакомым артикулом — и поставка
983:     # не уезжала, а кнопка была мертва. Поймать это легко: продавец добавил в
984:     # поставку в своём кабинете товар, которого нет в нашем каталоге.
985:     #
986:     # Теперь такой заказ ведёт себя как отменённый: он исключается из списания
987:     # со склада, показывается предупреждением и передаче не мешает. Списать
988:     # товар, которого мы не знаем, всё равно невозможно, а держать из-за него
989:     # весь склад — нельзя.
990:     plan = await source_svc.plan_fbs_shipment_sources(
991:         session,
992:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
993:         supply_warehouse_id=supply.warehouse_id,
994:         requests=[
995:             source_svc.FbsShipmentSourceRequest(
996:                 fbs_order_id=order.id,
997:                 product_id=order.product_id,
998:                 quantity=1,
999:             )
1000:             for order in orders
1001:             if order.product_id is not None
1002:         ],
1003:     )
1004:     return orders, composition, plan
1005: 
1006: 
1007: async def _sync_and_validate_deliver(
1008:     session: AsyncSession,
1009:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1010:     supply: FbsSupply,
1011:     http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
1012:     token: str,
1013:     *,
1014:     confirmed_preflight_version: str | None = None,
1015:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1016: ) -> tuple[list[FbsOrder], bool, source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan]:
1017:     orders, composition, source_plan = await _actual_wb_orders_and_source_plan(
1018:         session, tenant_id, supply, http_client, token, actor_user_id=actor_user_id
1019:     )
1020:     cargo_qr_ready = True
1021:     if supply.delivery_type == FBS_DELIVERY_TYPE_PVZ:
1022:         cargo_qr_ready = await pvz_svc.supply_has_ready_cargo_place_qrs(session, tenant_id, supply)
1023:     box_readiness = await packing_box_svc.get_delivery_box_readiness(
1024:         session, tenant_id, supply.id, orders
1025:     )
1026:     if confirmed_preflight_version is not None:
1027:         current_version = _compute_preflight_version(
1028:             supply,
1029:             orders,
1030:             cargo_qr_ready=cargo_qr_ready,
1031:             has_physical_boxes=box_readiness.has_physical_boxes,
1032:             without_distribution=box_readiness.without_distribution,
1033:             unassigned_packed_order_ids=box_readiness.unassigned_packed_order_ids,
1034:             composition_fingerprint=composition.wb_order_fingerprint,
1035:             source_plan=source_plan,
1036:         )
1037:         if current_version != confirmed_preflight_version:
1038:             raise FbsShipmentError(
1039:                 "stale_preflight",
1040:                 message=_STALE_PREFLIGHT_MESSAGE,
1041:                 context={
1042:                     "current_version": current_version,
1043:                     "confirmed_preflight_version": confirmed_preflight_version,
1044:                 },
1045:                 http_status=409,
1046:             )
1047:     checks = _build_delivery_checks(
1048:         supply,
1049:         orders,
1050:         cargo_qr_ready=cargo_qr_ready,
1051:         has_physical_boxes=box_readiness.has_physical_boxes,
1052:         without_distribution=box_readiness.without_distribution,
1053:         unassigned_packed_order_ids=box_readiness.unassigned_packed_order_ids,
1054:         discrepancies=composition.discrepancies,
1055:         source_plan=source_plan,
1056:     )
1057:     _validate_checks_pass(checks)
1058:     current_version = _compute_preflight_version(
1059:         supply,
1060:         orders,
1061:         cargo_qr_ready=cargo_qr_ready,
1062:         has_physical_boxes=box_readiness.has_physical_boxes,
1063:         without_distribution=box_readiness.without_distribution,
1064:         unassigned_packed_order_ids=box_readiness.unassigned_packed_order_ids,
1065:         composition_fingerprint=composition.wb_order_fingerprint,
1066:         source_plan=source_plan,
1067:     )
1068:     if source_plan.has_shortage and confirmed_preflight_version != current_version:
1069:         raise FbsShipmentError(
1070:             "negative_stock_confirmation_required",
1071:             message=(
1072:                 "Проверки не подтверждены, а остатка не хватает. Закройте окно "
1073:                 "передачи и откройте заново — система покажет, какой заказ "
1074:                 "уйдёт в минус, и спросит подтверждение."
1075:             ),
1076:             context={"current_version": current_version},
1077:             http_status=409,
1078:         )
1079:     return orders, cargo_qr_ready, source_plan
1080: 
1081: 
1082: def _meta_validation_context(exc: WildberriesBusinessError) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
1083:     return [
1084:         {
1085:             "order_id": item.order_id,
1086:             "key": item.key,
1087:             "value": item.value,
1088:             "decision": item.decision,
1089:             "reason": item.reason,
1090:         }
1091:         for item in exc.meta_validation
1092:     ]
1093: 
1094: 
1095: async def preflight_delivery(
1096:     session: AsyncSession,
1097:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1098:     supply_id: uuid.UUID,
1099:     http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
1100:     *,
1101:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1102: ) -> DeliveryPreflightResult:
1103:     supply = await _get_supply_read(session, tenant_id, supply_id, with_trbxes=True)
1104:     if supply is None:
1105:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
1106:     if supply.delivery_type not in _DELIVER_ALLOWED_DELIVERY_TYPES:
1107:         raise FbsShipmentError("wrong_delivery_type")
1108: 
1109:     if supply.marketplace == "ozon":
1110:         await _ozon_credentials(session, tenant_id, supply.seller_id)
1111:         orders = await _load_supply_orders_read(session, tenant_id, supply.id)
1112:         try:
1113:             source_plan = await plan_ozon_shipment_sources(
1114:                 session, tenant_id=tenant_id, warehouse_id=supply.warehouse_id, orders=orders,
1115:             )
1116:         except OzonPackagingError as exc:
1117:             raise FbsShipmentError(str(exc), http_status=409) from exc
1118:         checks = _build_delivery_checks(
1119:             supply,
1120:             orders,
1121:             cargo_qr_ready=True,
1122:             boxes_required=False,
1123:             source_plan=source_plan,
1124:         )
1125:         checked_at = datetime.now(UTC)
1126:         version = _compute_preflight_version(
1127:             supply,
1128:             orders,
1129:             cargo_qr_ready=True,
1130:             has_physical_boxes=False,
1131:             without_distribution=False,
1132:             unassigned_packed_order_ids=frozenset(),
1133:             source_plan=source_plan,
1134:         )
1135:         return DeliveryPreflightResult(
1136:             can_deliver=_checks_allow_delivery(checks),
1137:             version=version,
1138:             checked_at=checked_at,
1139:             checks=tuple(checks),
1140:         )
1141: 
1142:     token = await _require_marketplace_token(session, tenant_id, supply.seller_id)
1143:     orders, composition, source_plan = await _actual_wb_orders_and_source_plan(
1144:         session, tenant_id, supply, http_client, token, actor_user_id=actor_user_id
1145:     )
1146:     cargo_qr_ready = True
1147:     if supply.delivery_type == FBS_DELIVERY_TYPE_PVZ:
1148:         cargo_qr_ready = await pvz_svc.supply_has_ready_cargo_place_qrs(session, tenant_id, supply)
1149:     box_readiness = await packing_box_svc.get_delivery_box_readiness(
1150:         session, tenant_id, supply.id, orders
1151:     )
1152: 
1153:     checks = _build_delivery_checks(
1154:         supply,
1155:         orders,
1156:         cargo_qr_ready=cargo_qr_ready,
1157:         has_physical_boxes=box_readiness.has_physical_boxes,
1158:         without_distribution=box_readiness.without_distribution,
1159:         unassigned_packed_order_ids=box_readiness.unassigned_packed_order_ids,
1160:         discrepancies=composition.discrepancies,
1161:         source_plan=source_plan,
1162:     )
1163:     checked_at = datetime.now(UTC)
1164:     version = _compute_preflight_version(
1165:         supply,
1166:         orders,
1167:         cargo_qr_ready=cargo_qr_ready,
1168:         has_physical_boxes=box_readiness.has_physical_boxes,
1169:         without_distribution=box_readiness.without_distribution,
1170:         unassigned_packed_order_ids=box_readiness.unassigned_packed_order_ids,
1171:         composition_fingerprint=composition.wb_order_fingerprint,
1172:         source_plan=source_plan,
1173:     )
1174:     can_deliver = _checks_allow_delivery(checks)
1175:     from app.services.fbs_cancelled_after_pack_service import delivery_cancelled_orders
1176: 
1177:     return DeliveryPreflightResult(
1178:         can_deliver=can_deliver,
1179:         version=version,
1180:         checked_at=checked_at,
1181:         checks=tuple(checks),
1182:         cancelled_orders=tuple(await delivery_cancelled_orders(session, supply)),
1183:     )
1184: 
1185: 
1186: def delivery_preflight_to_dict(result: DeliveryPreflightResult) -> dict[str, Any]:
1187:     return {
1188:         "can_deliver": result.can_deliver,
1189:         "version": result.version,
1190:         "checked_at": result.checked_at.isoformat(),
1191:         "checks": _checks_to_payload(list(result.checks)),
1192:         "cancelled_orders": list(result.cancelled_orders),
1193:     }
1194: 
1195: 
1196: async def _apply_local_delivered(
1197:     session: AsyncSession,
1198:     supply: FbsSupply,
1199:     orders: list[FbsOrder],
1200:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1201:     source_plan: source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan | None,
1202:     operation: Any,
1203: ) -> None:
1204:     await _write_off_delivered_orders_once(
1205:         session,
1206:         supply,
1207:         orders,
1208:         actor_user_id,
1209:         source_plan=source_plan,
1210:         operation=operation,
1211:     )
1212:     now = supply.delivered_at or getattr(operation, "confirmed_at", None) or datetime.now(UTC)
1213:     supply.status = FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_IN_DELIVERY
1214:     supply.delivered_at = now
1215:     for order in orders:
1216:         if order.status not in _TERMINAL_ORDER_STATUSES:
1217:             order.status = FBS_ORDER_STATUS_IN_DELIVERY
1218:     await session.flush()
1219: 
1220:     from app.services.fbs_order_billing_service import charge_handed_over_orders
1221: 
1222:     await charge_handed_over_orders(session, orders, occurred_at=now)
1223: 
1224: 
1225: async def _write_off_delivered_orders_once(
1226:     session: AsyncSession,
1227:     supply: FbsSupply,
1228:     orders: list[FbsOrder],
1229:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1230:     *,
1231:     source_plan: source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan | None,
1232:     operation: Any,
1233: ) -> None:
1234:     """Create exactly one physical write-off recipe per confirmed FBS order."""
1235:     active_orders = [
1236:         order
1237:         for order in orders
1238:         if order.status != FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED
1239:         and (order.product_id is not None or order.marketplace == "ozon")
1240:     ]
1241:     order_ids = [order.id for order in active_orders]
1242:     existing_ledgers = {
1243:         ledger.fbs_order_id: ledger
1244:         for ledger in (
1245:             (
1246:                 await session.execute(
1247:                     select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger)
1248:                     .where(
1249:                         FbsShipmentReversalLedger.tenant_id == supply.tenant_id,
1250:                         FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id.in_(order_ids),
1251:                     )
1252:                     .with_for_update()
1253:                 )
1254:             )
1255:             .scalars()
1256:             .all()
1257:         )
1258:     }
1259:     resolutions = {
1260:         item.fbs_order_id: item
1261:         for item in (source_plan.resolutions if source_plan is not None else ())
1262:     }
1263: 
1264:     for order in active_orders:
1265:         ledger = existing_ledgers.get(order.id)
1266:         if order.marketplace == "ozon" and (
1267:             ledger is None or (ledger.shipment_movement_id is None and ledger.ozon_positions_json)
1268:         ):
1269:             try:
1270:                 ledger = await write_off_ozon_order(
1271:                     session,
1272:                     tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1273:                     order=order,
1274:                     actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1275:                     ledger=ledger,
1276:                 )
1277:             except OzonPackagingError as exc:
1278:                 raise FbsShipmentError(str(exc), http_status=409) from exc
1279:             existing_ledgers[order.id] = ledger
1280:         if ledger is None:
1281:             if order.product_id is None:
1282:                 # Сюда доходит только Ozon: заказы WB без сопоставленного товара
1283:                 # отсеяны фильтром active_orders выше — для них решение владельца
1284:                 # уже применено, они уезжают с предупреждением и без списания.
1285:                 #
1286:                 # У Ozon такого решения нет, и молча отпускать поставку без
1287:                 # списания нельзя: товар уедет физически, а в системе останется,
1288:                 # и найти это расхождение будет нечем — ledger не создаётся.
1289:                 raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_product_missing", http_status=409)
1290:             resolution = resolutions.get(order.id)
1291:             if resolution is None and order.marketplace == "ozon":
1292:                 sorting = await get_or_create_sorting_location(
1293:                     session,
1294:                     supply.tenant_id,
1295:                     supply.warehouse_id,
1296:                 )
1297:                 ledger = FbsShipmentReversalLedger(
1298:                     tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1299:                     fbs_order_id=order.id,
1300:                     product_id=order.product_id,
1301:                     storage_location_id=sorting.id,
1302:                     source_warehouse_id=supply.warehouse_id,
1303:                     source_mode="legacy_sorting",
1304:                     quantity=1,
1305:                     wb_operation_id=operation.id,
1306:                     written_off_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
1307:                 )
1308:                 session.add(ledger)
1309:                 await session.flush()
1310:                 existing_ledgers[order.id] = ledger
1311:             elif resolution is None:
1312:                 raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_source_missing", http_status=409)
1313:             else:
1314:                 ledger = FbsShipmentReversalLedger(
1315:                     tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1316:                     fbs_order_id=order.id,
1317:                     product_id=order.product_id,
1318:                     storage_location_id=resolution.storage_location_id,
1319:                     source_warehouse_id=resolution.source_warehouse_id,
1320:                     container_kind=resolution.container_kind,
1321:                     container_id=resolution.container_id,
1322:                     source_mode=resolution.source_mode,
1323:                     quantity=1,
1324:                     shortage_quantity=resolution.shortage_quantity,
1325:                     negative_quantity=resolution.negative_quantity,
1326:                     wb_operation_id=operation.id,
1327:                     written_off_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
1328:                 )
1329:                 session.add(ledger)
1330:                 await session.flush()
1331:                 existing_ledgers[order.id] = ledger
1332: 
1333:         if ledger.reversed_at is not None:
1334:             raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_already_reversed", http_status=409)
1335:         resolution = resolutions.get(order.id)
1336:         if ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None and ledger.source_mode is None:
1337:             # A pre-migration ledger already points at the place actually written
1338:             # off.  Do not decorate it with a newly recalculated container/source.
1339:             ledger.source_mode = "legacy_ledger"
1340:         if ledger.wb_operation_id is None:
1341:             ledger.wb_operation_id = operation.id
1342:         if ledger.written_off_by_user_id is None:
1343:             ledger.written_off_by_user_id = actor_user_id
1344:         if ledger.written_off_at is None and ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None:
1345:             ledger.written_off_at = datetime.now(UTC)
1346:         if ledger.shipment_movement_id is None:
1347:             staged_source = (
1348:                 ledger.source_mode is not None and ledger.source_warehouse_id is not None
1349:             )
1350:             if not staged_source and resolution is None:
1351:                 raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_source_missing", http_status=409)
1352:             if not staged_source:
1353:                 assert resolution is not None
1354:                 ledger.product_id = resolution.product_id
1355:                 ledger.storage_location_id = resolution.storage_location_id
1356:                 ledger.source_warehouse_id = resolution.source_warehouse_id
1357:                 ledger.container_kind = resolution.container_kind
1358:                 ledger.container_id = resolution.container_id
1359:                 ledger.source_mode = resolution.source_mode
1360:                 ledger.shortage_quantity = resolution.shortage_quantity
1361:                 ledger.negative_quantity = resolution.negative_quantity
1362:             allow_negative = int(ledger.negative_quantity) > 0
1363:             container_kind = cast(ContainerKind | None, ledger.container_kind)
1364:             try:
1365:                 async with session.begin_nested():
1366:                     movement = await inventory_svc.apply_fbs_supply_write_off(
1367:                         session,
1368:                         fbs_order_id=order.id,
1369:                         tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1370:                         product_id=ledger.product_id,
1371:                         storage_location_id=ledger.storage_location_id,
1372:                         quantity=int(ledger.quantity),
1373:                         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1374:                         allow_negative=allow_negative,
1375:                         container_kind=container_kind,
1376:                         container_id=ledger.container_id,
1377:                     )
1378:             except ValueError as exc:
1379:                 if allow_negative or str(exc) != "insufficient stock":
1380:                     raise
1381:                 movement = await inventory_svc.apply_fbs_supply_write_off(
1382:                     session,
1383:                     fbs_order_id=order.id,
1384:                     tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1385:                     product_id=ledger.product_id,
1386:                     storage_location_id=ledger.storage_location_id,
1387:                     quantity=int(ledger.quantity),
1388:                     actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1389:                     allow_negative=True,
1390:                     container_kind=container_kind,
1391:                     container_id=ledger.container_id,
1392:                 )
1393:                 ledger.shortage_quantity = int(ledger.quantity)
1394:                 ledger.negative_quantity = int(ledger.quantity)
1395:             await session.flush()
1396:             ledger.shipment_movement_id = movement.id
1397:             ledger.wb_operation_id = operation.id
1398:             ledger.written_off_by_user_id = actor_user_id
1399:             ledger.written_off_at = datetime.now(UTC)
1400:         await _release_reservation(session, order)
1401:     await session.flush()
1402: 
1403: 
1404: async def _stage_wb_shipment_sources(
1405:     session: AsyncSession,
1406:     supply: FbsSupply,
1407:     orders: list[FbsOrder],
1408:     source_plan: source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan,
1409:     operation: Any,
1410:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1411: ) -> None:
1412:     """Durably snapshot an attempt and prepare the physical write-off ledger."""
1413:     resolutions = {item.fbs_order_id: item for item in source_plan.resolutions}
1414:     active_orders = [
1415:         order
1416:         for order in orders
1417:         if order.status not in _TERMINAL_ORDER_STATUSES and order.product_id is not None
1418:     ]
1419:     # ⛔ Раньше здесь стоял ранний выход: нет заказов со сопоставленным товаром —
1420:     # чек-пойнт не пишем. А дальше передача этот чек-пойнт читает, не находит и
1421:     # падает с «не удалось восстановить план списания». Поставка из одного
1422:     # заказа с незнакомым артикулом — обычное дело, когда состав собрал сам
1423:     # продавец, — упиралась в мёртвую кнопку навсегда.
1424:     #
1425:     # Пустой план списания — законное состояние: списывать нечего, но передать
1426:     # надо. Пишем чек-пойнт с пустым списком и уходим.
1427:     checkpoint_source_plan = {
1428:         "supply_warehouse_id": str(source_plan.supply_warehouse_id),
1429:         "resolutions": [
1430:             {
1431:                 "fbs_order_id": str(item.fbs_order_id),
1432:                 "product_id": str(item.product_id),
1433:                 "quantity": item.quantity,
1434:                 "source_warehouse_id": str(item.source_warehouse_id),
1435:                 "storage_location_id": str(item.storage_location_id),
1436:                 "container_kind": item.container_kind,
1437:                 "container_id": str(item.container_id) if item.container_id else None,
1438:                 "source_mode": item.source_mode,
1439:                 "positive_quantity": item.positive_quantity,
1440:                 "shortage_quantity": item.shortage_quantity,
1441:                 "negative_quantity": item.negative_quantity,
1442:             }
1443:             for item in sorted(source_plan.resolutions, key=lambda row: str(row.fbs_order_id))
1444:         ],
1445:     }
1446:     summary = dict(operation.request_summary_json or {})
1447:     previous_checkpoint = summary.get("checkpoint_source_plan")
1448:     if previous_checkpoint is not None and previous_checkpoint != checkpoint_source_plan:
1449:         raise FbsShipmentError(
1450:             "stale_preflight",
1451:             message=_STALE_PREFLIGHT_MESSAGE,
1452:             http_status=409,
1453:         )
1454:     summary["checkpoint_source_plan"] = checkpoint_source_plan
1455:     operation.request_summary_json = summary
1456:     existing = {
1457:         ledger.fbs_order_id: ledger
1458:         for ledger in (
1459:             (
1460:                 await session.execute(
1461:                     select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger)
1462:                     .where(
1463:                         FbsShipmentReversalLedger.tenant_id == supply.tenant_id,
1464:                         FbsShipmentReversalLedger.fbs_order_id.in_(
1465:                             [order.id for order in active_orders]
1466:                         ),
1467:                     )
1468:                     .with_for_update()
1469:                 )
1470:             )
1471:             .scalars()
1472:             .all()
1473:         )
1474:     }
1475:     for order in active_orders:
1476:         resolution = resolutions.get(order.id)
1477:         if resolution is None:
1478:             raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_source_missing", http_status=409)
1479:         ledger = existing.get(order.id)
1480:         if ledger is None:
1481:             ledger = FbsShipmentReversalLedger(
1482:                 tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1483:                 fbs_order_id=order.id,
1484:                 product_id=resolution.product_id,
1485:                 storage_location_id=resolution.storage_location_id,
1486:                 source_warehouse_id=resolution.source_warehouse_id,
1487:                 container_kind=resolution.container_kind,
1488:                 container_id=resolution.container_id,
1489:                 source_mode=resolution.source_mode,
1490:                 quantity=resolution.quantity,
1491:                 shortage_quantity=resolution.shortage_quantity,
1492:                 negative_quantity=resolution.negative_quantity,
1493:                 wb_operation_id=operation.id,
1494:                 written_off_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
1495:             )
1496:             session.add(ledger)
1497:         elif ledger.shipment_movement_id is None:
1498:             # The immutable audit checkpoint lives in operation.request_summary.
1499:             # This single per-order ledger is the eventual physical write-off
1500:             # row, so a new definitive attempt prepares it from its own frozen
1501:             # snapshot without changing any prior operation's snapshot.
1502:             ledger.product_id = resolution.product_id
1503:             ledger.storage_location_id = resolution.storage_location_id
1504:             ledger.source_warehouse_id = resolution.source_warehouse_id
1505:             ledger.container_kind = resolution.container_kind
1506:             ledger.container_id = resolution.container_id
1507:             ledger.source_mode = resolution.source_mode
1508:             ledger.quantity = resolution.quantity
1509:             ledger.shortage_quantity = resolution.shortage_quantity
1510:             ledger.negative_quantity = resolution.negative_quantity
1511:             ledger.wb_operation_id = operation.id
1512:         elif ledger.wb_operation_id is None:
1513:             ledger.wb_operation_id = operation.id
1514:         if ledger.written_off_by_user_id is None:
1515:             ledger.written_off_by_user_id = actor_user_id
1516:     await session.flush()
1517: 
1518: 
1519: async def _load_checkpointed_wb_delivery(
1520:     session: AsyncSession,
1521:     supply: FbsSupply,
1522:     operation: Any,
1523: ) -> tuple[list[FbsOrder], source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan] | None:
1524:     """Load the durable WB source plan after marketplace confirmation."""
1525:     summary = operation.request_summary_json or {}
1526:     checkpoint_source_plan = summary.get("checkpoint_source_plan")
1527:     if isinstance(checkpoint_source_plan, dict):
1528:         raw_resolutions = checkpoint_source_plan.get("resolutions")
1529:         # Пустой список — это «списывать нечего», а не битый чек-пойнт. Так
1530:         # выглядит поставка, в которой ни один заказ не сопоставлен с карточкой
1531:         # товара: уехать она обязана, движений по складу не создаст.
1532:         if not isinstance(raw_resolutions, list):
1533:             raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_checkpoint_incomplete", http_status=409)
1534:         orders = await _load_locked_supply_orders(session, supply.tenant_id, supply.id)
1535:         orders_by_id = {order.id: order for order in orders}
1536:         snapshot_orders: list[FbsOrder] = []
1537:         snapshot_resolutions: list[source_svc.FbsShipmentSourceResolution] = []
1538:         try:
1539:             supply_warehouse_id = uuid.UUID(str(checkpoint_source_plan["supply_warehouse_id"]))
1540:             for raw in raw_resolutions:
1541:                 if not isinstance(raw, dict):
1542:                     raise ValueError("invalid checkpoint row")
1543:                 order_id = uuid.UUID(str(raw["fbs_order_id"]))
1544:                 # Заказ должен быть в поставке, но само его присутствие тут
1545:                 # больше не решает, какие заказы считать уехавшими: список «что
1546:                 # уехало» и список «что списываем» — разные вещи.
1547:                 orders_by_id[order_id]
1548:                 snapshot_resolutions.append(
1549:                     source_svc.FbsShipmentSourceResolution(
1550:                         fbs_order_id=order_id,
1551:                         product_id=uuid.UUID(str(raw["product_id"])),
1552:                         quantity=int(raw["quantity"]),
1553:                         source_warehouse_id=uuid.UUID(str(raw["source_warehouse_id"])),
1554:                         storage_location_id=uuid.UUID(str(raw["storage_location_id"])),
1555:                         container_kind=cast(ContainerKind | None, raw.get("container_kind")),
1556:                         container_id=(
1557:                             uuid.UUID(str(raw["container_id"])) if raw.get("container_id") else None
1558:                         ),
1559:                         source_mode=cast(source_svc.FbsShipmentSourceMode, raw["source_mode"]),
1560:                         positive_quantity=int(raw["positive_quantity"]),
1561:                         shortage_quantity=int(raw["shortage_quantity"]),
1562:                         negative_quantity=int(raw["negative_quantity"]),
1563:                     )
1564:                 )
1565:         except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
1566:             raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_checkpoint_incomplete", http_status=409) from None
1567:         # Уехало всё, что лежало в поставке и не отменено, — включая заказы без
1568:         # сопоставленного товара, по которым списывать нечего. Раньше сюда
1569:         # попадали только строки плана списания, и несопоставленный заказ
1570:         # оставался «упакован» внутри уже переданной поставки: оператор не мог
1571:         # понять, уехал он или нет. Хуже того, при повторном подтверждении план
1572:         # пересчитывался заново и тот же заказ статус получал — одно и то же
1573:         # вело себя по-разному в зависимости от пути.
1574:         snapshot_orders = [
1575:             order for order in orders if order.status not in _TERMINAL_ORDER_STATUSES
1576:         ]
1577:         return snapshot_orders, source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan(
1578:             tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1579:             supply_warehouse_id=supply_warehouse_id,
1580:             resolutions=tuple(snapshot_resolutions),
1581:         )
1582: 
1583:     # Backward compatibility for operations created before journal snapshots.
1584:     ledgers = list(
1585:         (
1586:             await session.execute(
1587:                 select(FbsShipmentReversalLedger).where(
1588:                     FbsShipmentReversalLedger.tenant_id == supply.tenant_id,
1589:                     FbsShipmentReversalLedger.wb_operation_id == operation.id,
1590:                 )
1591:             )
1592:         )
1593:         .scalars()
1594:         .all()
1595:     )
1596:     if not ledgers:
1597:         return None
1598:     orders = await _load_locked_supply_orders(session, supply.tenant_id, supply.id)
1599:     orders_by_id = {order.id: order for order in orders}
1600:     checkpointed_orders: list[FbsOrder] = []
1601:     resolutions: list[source_svc.FbsShipmentSourceResolution] = []
1602:     for ledger in ledgers:
1603:         ledger_order = orders_by_id.get(ledger.fbs_order_id)
1604:         source_warehouse_id = ledger.source_warehouse_id
1605:         source_mode = ledger.source_mode
1606:         if ledger.shipment_movement_id is not None and source_warehouse_id is None:
1607:             source_warehouse_id = await session.scalar(
1608:                 select(StorageLocation.warehouse_id).where(
1609:                     StorageLocation.id == ledger.storage_location_id,
1610:                     StorageLocation.tenant_id == supply.tenant_id,
1611:                 )
1612:             )
1613:             source_mode = source_mode or "legacy_ledger"
1614:         if ledger_order is None or source_warehouse_id is None or source_mode is None:
1615:             raise FbsShipmentError(
1616:                 "fbs_shipment_checkpoint_incomplete",
1617:                 context={"order_id": str(ledger.fbs_order_id)},
1618:                 http_status=409,
1619:             )
1620:         checkpointed_orders.append(ledger_order)
1621:         quantity = int(ledger.quantity)
1622:         shortage = int(ledger.shortage_quantity)
1623:         resolutions.append(
1624:             source_svc.FbsShipmentSourceResolution(
1625:                 fbs_order_id=ledger.fbs_order_id,
1626:                 product_id=ledger.product_id,
1627:                 quantity=quantity,
1628:                 source_warehouse_id=source_warehouse_id,
1629:                 storage_location_id=ledger.storage_location_id,
1630:                 container_kind=cast(ContainerKind | None, ledger.container_kind),
1631:                 container_id=ledger.container_id,
1632:                 source_mode=cast(source_svc.FbsShipmentSourceMode, source_mode),
1633:                 positive_quantity=max(0, quantity - shortage),
1634:                 shortage_quantity=shortage,
1635:                 negative_quantity=int(ledger.negative_quantity),
1636:             )
1637:         )
1638:     return checkpointed_orders, source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan(
1639:         tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1640:         supply_warehouse_id=supply.warehouse_id,
1641:         resolutions=tuple(resolutions),
1642:     )
1643: 
1644: 
1645: async def _fetch_supply_qr_after_deliver(
1646:     session: AsyncSession,
1647:     supply: FbsSupply,
1648:     http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
1649:     token: str,
1650: ) -> None:
1651:     # WB issues a supply QR (GET /api/v3/supplies/{id}/barcode) regardless of
1652:     # delivery_type — verified against the WB API for both warehouse_sc and pvz
1653:     # supplies on 2026-08-17. Do not re-add a delivery_type gate here.
1654:     if supply.barcode_file and supply.barcode_asset_id:
1655:         return
1656:     try:
1657:         png_bytes = await fetch_marketplace_supply_barcode(
1658:             http_client,
1659:             api_token=token,
1660:             supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
1661:         )
1662:     except WildberriesClientError as exc:
1663:         raise _shipment_error_from_wb(
1664:             exc,
1665:             tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1666:             seller_id=supply.seller_id,
1667:             local_entity_id=supply.id,
1668:             wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
1669:             event="fbs shipment WB supply QR fetch failed",
1670:         ) from exc
1671:     supply.barcode_file = _save_barcode_png(supply.id, png_bytes)
1672:     try:
1673:         await upsert_supply_qr_asset_from_bytes(
1674:             session,
1675:             tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1676:             supply=supply,
1677:             png_bytes=png_bytes,
1678:         )
1679:     except FbsPrintAssetStorageError as exc:
1680:         raise FbsShipmentError(exc.code) from exc
1681:     await session.flush()
1682: 
1683: 
1684: async def _persist_confirmed_delivery(
1685:     session: AsyncSession,
1686:     supply: FbsSupply,
1687:     orders: list[FbsOrder],
1688:     operation: Any,
1689:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1690:     source_plan: source_svc.FbsShipmentSourcePlan | None = None,
1691: ) -> None:
1692:     """Persist marketplace confirmation and the matching local stock result."""
1693:     confirmed_at = operation.confirmed_at
1694:     if source_plan is None:
1695:         # Ozon writes stock off at confirmed handover; its source recipe uses
1696:         # the historically named packaging ledger. Keep local stock and the
1697:         # operation confirmation in the same transaction.
1698:         await _apply_local_delivered(session, supply, orders, actor_user_id, source_plan, operation)
1699:         await mark_deliver_operation_confirmed(
1700:             session,
1701:             operation,
1702:             wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
1703:             local_supply_id=supply.id,
1704:         )
1705:         if confirmed_at is not None:
1706:             operation.confirmed_at = confirmed_at
1707:         await session.commit()
1708:         return
1709: 
1710:     # WB delivery cannot be rolled back.  Persist that fact first so any later
1711:     # inventory/container failure is recoverable by retrying the same
1712:     # idempotency key without ever sending a second deliver mutation to WB.
1713:     await _stage_wb_shipment_sources(
1714:         session,
1715:         supply,
1716:         orders,
1717:         source_plan,
1718:         operation,
1719:         actor_user_id,
1720:     )
1721:     await mark_deliver_operation_confirmed(
1722:         session,
1723:         operation,
1724:         wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
1725:         local_supply_id=supply.id,
1726:     )
1727:     # A local recovery must retain the first persisted marketplace confirmation.
1728:     if confirmed_at is not None:
1729:         operation.confirmed_at = confirmed_at
1730:     await session.commit()
1731:     await _apply_local_delivered(session, supply, orders, actor_user_id, source_plan, operation)
1732:     # The optional QR fetch happens after this second checkpoint as well.
1733:     await session.commit()
1734: 
1735: 
1736: async def _ozon_credentials(
1737:     session: AsyncSession,
1738:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1739:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
1740: ) -> tuple[str, str]:
1741:     try:
1742:         return await MarketplaceAccountService(session).stored_credentials(
1743:             tenant_id,
1744:             seller_id,
1745:         )
1746:     except MarketplaceAccountError as exc:
1747:         raise FbsShipmentError(
1748:             exc.code,
1749:             message="Кабинет Ozon не подключён.",
1750:             http_status=409,
1751:         ) from exc
1752: 
1753: 
1754: async def _store_ozon_supply_qr(
1755:     session: AsyncSession,
1756:     supply: FbsSupply,
1757:     provider: OzonMarketplaceProvider,
1758:     *,
1759:     client_id: str,
1760:     api_key: str,
1761: ) -> bytes:
1762:     external_supply_id = supply.external_supply_id or supply.wb_supply_id
1763:     try:
1764:         png_bytes = await provider.fetch_supply_qr(
1765:             client_id=client_id,
1766:             api_key=api_key,
1767:             supply_id=external_supply_id,
1768:         )
1769:     except MarketplaceProviderError as exc:
1770:         raise FbsShipmentError(
1771:             exc.code,
1772:             message=provider_error_message(exc),
1773:             retryable=exc.status_code in {429, 500, 502, 503, 504},
1774:             http_status=exc.status_code or 502,
1775:         ) from exc
1776:     if not png_bytes:
1777:         raise FbsShipmentError(
1778:             "ozon_supply_qr_missing",
1779:             message="Ozon не вернул QR поставки.",
1780:             http_status=502,
1781:         )
1782:     try:
1783:         await upsert_supply_qr_asset_from_bytes(
1784:             session,
1785:             tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
1786:             supply=supply,
1787:             png_bytes=png_bytes,
1788:         )
1789:     except FbsPrintAssetStorageError as exc:
1790:         raise FbsShipmentError(exc.code) from exc
1791:     await session.flush()
1792:     return png_bytes
1793: 
1794: 
1795: # Ключ снимка передачи Ozon в журнале операции. Рядом лежит вайлдберрисовский
1796: # `checkpoint_source_plan` — они не пересекаются: у каждого маркетплейса своя
1797: # точка сохранения и свой смысл «что уже необратимо сделано».
1798: OZON_HANDOFF_PROGRESS_KEY = "ozon_handoff_progress"
1799: 
1800: 
1801: async def _save_ozon_handoff_progress(
1802:     session: AsyncSession,
1803:     operation: Any,
1804:     progress: OzonHandoffProgress,
1805: ) -> None:
1806:     """Сделать снимок передачи Ozon долговечным.
1807: 
1808:     Коммит здесь обязателен и он же — весь смысл функции. Без него снимок
1809:     живёт только внутри открытой транзакции, а она при ошибке откатывается
1810:     целиком: то есть ровно в тот момент, ради которого снимок и заводился, он
1811:     исчезает.
1812:     """
1813:     summary = dict(operation.request_summary_json or {})
1814:     summary[OZON_HANDOFF_PROGRESS_KEY] = progress.to_json()
1815:     operation.request_summary_json = summary
1816:     await session.commit()
1817: 
1818: 
1819: async def _fail_ozon_deliver_operation(
1820:     session: AsyncSession,
1821:     operation: Any,
1822:     *,
1823:     error_code: str,
1824:     supply_id: uuid.UUID,
1825:     discard_local_changes: bool = False,
1826: ) -> None:
1827:     """Записать отказ передачи Ozon так, чтобы он пережил откат запроса.
1828: 
1829:     Раньше отказ помечался только `flush`, а коммита для озоновских кодов не
1830:     делал никто: запись операции пропадала вместе с транзакцией, и следующая
1831:     попытка не знала ни что уже уехало в кабинет, ни что предыдущая падала.
1832: 
1833:     `discard_local_changes` нужен там, где в сессии висит наполовину сделанная
1834:     локальная работа — списание, движения по складу, печатные активы. Такое
1835:     закоммитить вместе с отметкой об отказе нельзя: получится поставка,
1836:     списанная наполовину. Состояния самой передачи это не теряет — все её шаги
1837:     уже закоммичены снимками.
1838:     """
1839:     if discard_local_changes:
1840:         with suppress(SQLAlchemyError):
1841:             await session.rollback()
1842:         refreshed = await session.get(FbsWbOperation, operation.id)
1843:         if refreshed is None:
1844:             return
1845:         operation = refreshed
1846:     try:
1847:         await mark_operation_failed(
1848:             session,
1849:             operation,
1850:             error_code=error_code,
1851:             local_supply_id=supply_id,
1852:         )
1853:         await session.commit()
1854:         return
1855:     except SQLAlchemyError:
1856:         logger.exception(
1857:             "ozon deliver failure could not be journalled in place",
1858:             extra={"supply_id": str(supply_id), "error_code": error_code},
1859:         )
1860:     # Транзакция могла умереть вместе с исходной ошибкой. Тогда единственный
1861:     # способ сохранить отказ — начать чистую и перечитать саму операцию.
1862:     try:
1863:         await session.rollback()
1864:         fresh = await session.get(FbsWbOperation, operation.id)
1865:         if fresh is None:
1866:             return
1867:         await mark_operation_failed(
1868:             session,
1869:             fresh,
1870:             error_code=error_code,
1871:             local_supply_id=supply_id,
1872:         )
1873:         await session.commit()
1874:     except SQLAlchemyError:
1875:         logger.exception(
1876:             "ozon deliver failure could not be journalled at all",
1877:             extra={"supply_id": str(supply_id), "error_code": error_code},
1878:         )
1879: 
1880: 
1881: async def _deliver_ozon_supply(
1882:     session: AsyncSession,
1883:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1884:     supply_id: uuid.UUID,
1885:     *,
1886:     idempotency_key: str,
1887:     request_hash: str,
1888:     confirmed_preflight_version: str | None,
1889:     existing: Any,
1890:     provider: OzonMarketplaceProvider | None,
1891:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1892: ) -> FbsSupply:
1893:     supply = await _get_supply_read(session, tenant_id, supply_id)
1894:     if supply is None:
1895:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
1896:     # Keep the advisory lock on its own connection: checkpoints commit the
1897:     # business session and release its transaction/connection. A dead worker
1898:     # releases this lock automatically, so persisted PENDING is resumable.
1899:     async with (
1900:         AsyncSession(bind=session.bind) as lock_session,
1901:         marketplace_seller_lock(lock_session, supply.seller_id, "ozon-delivery") as acquired,
1902:     ):
1903:         if not acquired:
1904:             raise FbsShipmentError(
1905:                 "operation_in_progress",
1906:                 message="Передача в Ozon уже выполняется.",
1907:                 retryable=True,
1908:                 http_status=503,
1909:             )
1910:         if existing is not None:
1911:             await session.refresh(existing)
1912:         await session.refresh(supply)
1913:         return await _deliver_ozon_supply_locked(
1914:             session, tenant_id, supply_id,
1915:             idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
1916:             request_hash=request_hash,
1917:             confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
1918:             existing=existing,
1919:             provider=provider,
1920:             actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1921:         )
1922: 
1923: 
1924: async def _deliver_ozon_supply_locked(
1925:     session: AsyncSession,
1926:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1927:     supply_id: uuid.UUID,
1928:     *,
1929:     idempotency_key: str,
1930:     request_hash: str,
1931:     confirmed_preflight_version: str | None,
1932:     existing: Any,
1933:     provider: OzonMarketplaceProvider | None,
1934:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1935: ) -> FbsSupply:
1936:     supply = await _get_supply_for_update(
1937:         session,
1938:         tenant_id,
1939:         supply_id,
1940:         with_trbxes=True,
1941:     )
1942:     if supply is None:
1943:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
1944:     if supply.delivery_type not in _DELIVER_ALLOWED_DELIVERY_TYPES:
1945:         raise FbsShipmentError("wrong_delivery_type")
1946: 
1947:     client_id, api_key = await _ozon_credentials(
1948:         session,
1949:         tenant_id,
1950:         supply.seller_id,
1951:     )
1952:     if existing is not None:
1953:         if existing.request_hash and existing.request_hash != request_hash:
1954:             raise FbsShipmentError("idempotency_key_reused", http_status=409)
1955:         if existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED:
1956:             return supply
1957: 
1958:     if supply.status in _DELIVER_BLOCKED_SUPPLY_STATUSES:
1959:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_bad_status", http_status=409)
1960:     orders = await _load_locked_supply_orders(session, tenant_id, supply.id)
1961:     _validate_checks_pass(
1962:         _build_delivery_checks(
1963:             supply,
1964:             orders,
1965:             cargo_qr_ready=True,
1966:             boxes_required=False,
1967:         )
1968:     )
1969:     # Снимок собираем по всем попыткам этой поставки, а не по ключу
1970:     # идемпотентности: браузер после отказа обычно присылает новый ключ, и
1971:     # привязка только к нему потеряла бы всё, что уже сделано в кабинете.
1972:     progress = OzonHandoffProgress()
1973:     for attempt in await list_deliver_operations_for_supply(
1974:         session,
1975:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
1976:         seller_id=supply.seller_id,
1977:         local_supply_id=supply.id,
1978:     ):
1979:         if existing is None or attempt.id != existing.id:
1980:             if attempt.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED:
1981:                 return supply
1982:             if attempt.state in {
1983:                 WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING,
1984:                 WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
1985:             }:
1986:                 # No live owner holds the advisory lock. Reuse the original
1987:                 # operation even when the browser supplied a fresh retry key.
1988:                 if attempt.request_hash and attempt.request_hash != request_hash:
1989:                     raise FbsShipmentError("idempotency_key_reused", http_status=409)
1990:                 existing = attempt
1991:         progress.absorb(
1992:             OzonHandoffProgress.from_json(
1993:                 (attempt.request_summary_json or {}).get(OZON_HANDOFF_PROGRESS_KEY)
1994:             )
1995:         )
1996:     try:
1997:         source_plan = await plan_ozon_shipment_sources(
1998:             session, tenant_id=tenant_id, warehouse_id=supply.warehouse_id, orders=orders,
1999:         )
2000:         # A confirmed/ambiguous external handoff must finish local recovery;
2001:         # do not ask a new approval after the irreversible operation.
2002:         if not (progress.carriage_create_started or progress.carriage_id is not None
2003:                 or progress.used_fallback):
2004:             current_version = _compute_preflight_version(
2005:                 supply, orders, cargo_qr_ready=True, has_physical_boxes=False,
2006:                 without_distribution=False, unassigned_packed_order_ids=frozenset(),
2007:                 source_plan=source_plan,
2008:             )
2009:             if (confirmed_preflight_version is not None
2010:                     and confirmed_preflight_version != current_version):
2011:                 raise FbsShipmentError(
2012:                     "stale_preflight", message=_STALE_PREFLIGHT_MESSAGE,
2013:                     context={"current_version": current_version,
2014:                              "confirmed_preflight_version": confirmed_preflight_version},
2015:                     http_status=409,
2016:                 )
2017:             if source_plan.has_shortage and confirmed_preflight_version != current_version:
2018:                 raise FbsShipmentError(
2019:                     "negative_stock_confirmation_required",
2020:                     message="Остатка не хватает. Откройте окно передачи, проверьте "
2021:                             "предупреждение о минусе и подтвердите передачу.",
2022:                     context={"current_version": current_version}, http_status=409,
2023:                 )
2024:         await prepare_ozon_shipment_sources(
2025:             session, tenant_id=tenant_id, warehouse_id=supply.warehouse_id, orders=orders,
2026:             source_plan=source_plan,
2027:         )
2028:     except OzonPackagingError as exc:
2029:         raise FbsShipmentError(str(exc), http_status=409) from exc
2030:     if existing is not None:
2031:         operation = existing
2032:         operation.state = WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING
2033:         operation.error_code = None
2034:         operation.error_context_json = None
2035:         operation.failed_at = None
2036:     else:
2037:         operation = await create_pending_deliver_operation(
2038:             session,
2039:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
2040:             seller_id=supply.seller_id,
2041:             idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
2042:             request_hash=request_hash,
2043:             local_supply_id=supply.id,
2044:             confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
2045:         )
2046:     # Провайдер приходит из теста или из боевой настройки. Пока живой транспорт
2047:     # выключен, локальная операция сохраняется, а передача честно не выполняется:
2048:     # придумывать успех, которого не было, нельзя — товар физически не уехал.
2049:     if provider is None and ozon_live_api_enabled():
2050:         provider = build_ozon_provider()
2051:     if provider is None:
2052:         await _fail_ozon_deliver_operation(
2053:             session,
2054:             operation,
2055:             error_code="ozon_live_handoff_blocked",
2056:             supply_id=supply.id,
2057:         )
2058:         raise FbsShipmentError(
2059:             "ozon_live_handoff_blocked",
2060:             message=(
2061:                 "Передача в Ozon выключена настройкой: боевой транспорт Ozon не включён. "
2062:                 "Локальная складская операция сохранена."
2063:             ),
2064:             http_status=503,
2065:         )
2066: 
2067:     async def _checkpoint(state: OzonHandoffProgress) -> None:
2068:         await _save_ozon_handoff_progress(session, operation, state)
2069: 
2070:     # Первая долговечная точка — до любой мутации в кабинете. Без неё падение
2071:     # процесса стёрло бы саму запись о том, что передача начиналась.
2072:     await _checkpoint(progress)
2073:     try:
2074:         result = await handoff_supply(
2075:             session,
2076:             supply=supply,
2077:             orders=orders,
2078:             provider=provider,
2079:             client_id=client_id,
2080:             api_key=api_key,
2081:             progress=progress,
2082:             checkpoint=_checkpoint,
2083:         )
2084:     except OzonFbsProcessError as exc:
2085:         await _fail_ozon_deliver_operation(
2086:             session,
2087:             operation,
2088:             error_code=exc.code,
2089:             supply_id=supply.id,
2090:         )
2091:         raise FbsShipmentError(
2092:             exc.code,
2093:             message=exc.message,
2094:             retryable=False,
2095:             http_status=exc.status_code or 502,
2096:         ) from exc
2097:     except MarketplaceProviderError as exc:
2098:         await _fail_ozon_deliver_operation(
2099:             session,
2100:             operation,
2101:             error_code=exc.code,
2102:             supply_id=supply.id,
2103:         )
2104:         raise FbsShipmentError(
2105:             exc.code,
2106:             message=provider_error_message(exc),
2107:             retryable=exc.status_code in {429, 500, 502, 503, 504},
2108:             http_status=exc.status_code or 502,
2109:         ) from exc
2110: 
2111:     try:
2112:         return await _finish_ozon_delivery(
2113:             session,
2114:             supply=supply,
2115:             orders=orders,
2116:             operation=operation,
2117:             result=result,
2118:             actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
2119:         )
2120:     except Exception as exc:
2121:         # Передача в кабинете уже состоялась, а локальная часть сорвалась.
2122:         # Отметить отказ обязательно: иначе операция навсегда остаётся
2123:         # «выполняется» и повтор упирается в 503, хотя продолжить можно.
2124:         await _fail_ozon_deliver_operation(
2125:             session,
2126:             operation,
2127:             error_code=getattr(exc, "code", None) or "ozon_local_delivery_failed",
2128:             supply_id=supply.id,
2129:             discard_local_changes=True,
2130:         )
2131:         raise
2132: 
2133: 
2134: async def _finish_ozon_delivery(
2135:     session: AsyncSession,
2136:     *,
2137:     supply: FbsSupply,
2138:     orders: list[FbsOrder],
2139:     operation: Any,
2140:     result: OzonHandoffResult,
2141:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
2142: ) -> FbsSupply:
2143:     """Локальная часть уже состоявшейся передачи Ozon."""
2144:     # External checkpoints commit and release the initial row locks. Reacquire
2145:     # supply -> orders and refresh stale identity-map objects before write-off,
2146:     # matching picking/cancellation without holding a row lock across HTTP.
2147:     with session.no_autoflush:
2148:         await session.refresh(supply, with_for_update=True)
2149:     orders = list((await session.scalars(
2150:         _supply_orders_stmt(supply.tenant_id, supply.id)
2151:         .with_for_update()
2152:         .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
2153:     )).all())
2154:     supply.external_supply_id = str(result.carriage_id) if result.carriage_id is not None else None
2155:     supply.document_number = str(result.carriage_id) if result.carriage_id is not None else None
2156:     supply.display_number = result.barcode_text
2157:     if result.shipping_list_bytes:
2158:         # Лист отгрузки Ozon приходит в PDF, а хранилище печатных активов
2159:         # принимает только PNG, поэтому показать его оператору сегодня негде.
2160:         # Молча выбрасывать документ, за которым ехали в Ozon, нельзя —
2161:         # пусть его получение хотя бы видно в журнале.
2162:         logger.info(
2163:             "ozon shipping list received but not stored: no PDF surface yet",
2164:             extra={
2165:                 "supply_id": str(supply.id),
2166:                 "carriage_id": result.carriage_id,
2167:                 "bytes": len(result.shipping_list_bytes),
2168:             },
2169:         )
2170:     if result.barcode_bytes:
2171:         try:
2172:             await upsert_supply_qr_asset_from_bytes(
2173:                 session,
2174:                 tenant_id=supply.tenant_id,
2175:                 supply=supply,
2176:                 png_bytes=result.barcode_bytes,
2177:             )
2178:         except FbsPrintAssetStorageError as exc:
2179:             raise FbsShipmentError(exc.code) from exc
2180:     await _persist_confirmed_delivery(session, supply, orders, operation, actor_user_id)
2181:     return supply
2182: 
2183: 
2184: async def deliver_supply(
2185:     session: AsyncSession,
2186:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
2187:     supply_id: uuid.UUID,
2188:     http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
2189:     *,
2190:     idempotency_key: str,
2191:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
2192:     confirmed_preflight_version: str | None = None,
2193:     ozon_provider: OzonMarketplaceProvider | None = None,
2194: ) -> FbsSupply:
2195:     if not idempotency_key.strip():
2196:         raise FbsShipmentError("missing_idempotency_key")
2197: 
2198:     supply_read = await _get_supply_read(session, tenant_id, supply_id, with_trbxes=True)
2199:     if supply_read is None:
2200:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
2201: 
2202:     request_hash = request_hash_for_deliver(
2203:         supply_id=supply_id,
2204:         confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
2205:     )
2206:     existing_by_key = await get_deliver_operation_by_idempotency(
2207:         session, supply_read.seller_id, idempotency_key
2208:     )
2209:     if (
2210:         existing_by_key is not None
2211:         and existing_by_key.local_entity_type == "fbs_supply"
2212:         and existing_by_key.local_entity_id is not None
2213:         and existing_by_key.local_entity_id != supply_id
2214:     ):
2215:         raise FbsShipmentError("idempotency_key_reused", http_status=409)
2216:     existing = existing_by_key
2217:     if supply_read.marketplace == "ozon":
2218:         return await _deliver_ozon_supply(
2219:             session,
2220:             tenant_id,
2221:             supply_id,
2222:             idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
2223:             request_hash=request_hash,
2224:             confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
2225:             existing=existing,
2226:             provider=ozon_provider,
2227:             actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
2228:         )
2229:     if existing_by_key is not None and existing_by_key.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_FAILED:
2230:         # A closed client key has no authority over a newer supply-scoped
2231:         # attempt. Reject it before resolving the active operation, otherwise
2232:         # a stale tab could fail or reconcile another key's durable journal.
2233:         raise FbsShipmentError("idempotency_key_reused", http_status=409)
2234:     resumable_operation: Any | None = None
2235:     if existing is None or existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_FAILED:
2236:         active_for_supply = await get_active_deliver_operation_for_supply(
2237:             session,
2238:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
2239:             seller_id=supply_read.seller_id,
2240:             local_supply_id=supply_id,
2241:         )
2242:         if active_for_supply is not None:
2243:             existing = active_for_supply
2244:     if existing is not None:
2245:         if (
2246:             existing_by_key is not None
2247:             and existing.id == existing_by_key.id
2248:             and existing.request_hash
2249:             and existing.request_hash != request_hash
2250:         ):
2251:             raise FbsShipmentError("idempotency_key_reused", http_status=409)
2252:         if (
2253:             existing_by_key is not None
2254:             and existing.id == existing_by_key.id
2255:             and existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_FAILED
2256:         ):
2257:             raise FbsShipmentError("idempotency_key_reused", http_status=409)
2258:         if existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED:
2259:             confirmed_supply = await _get_supply_for_update(
2260:                 session, tenant_id, supply_id, with_trbxes=True
2261:             )
2262:             if confirmed_supply is not None:
2263:                 token = await _require_marketplace_token(
2264:                     session, tenant_id, confirmed_supply.seller_id
2265:                 )
2266:                 checkpointed = await _load_checkpointed_wb_delivery(
2267:                     session, confirmed_supply, existing
2268:                 )
2269:                 if checkpointed is None:
2270:                     orders, _, source_plan = await _actual_wb_orders_and_source_plan(
2271:                         session,
2272:                         tenant_id,
2273:                         confirmed_supply,
2274:                         http_client,
2275:                         token,
2276:                         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
2277:                     )
2278:                 else:
2279:                     orders, source_plan = checkpointed
2280:                 await _persist_confirmed_delivery(
2281:                     session,
2282:                     confirmed_supply,
2283:                     orders,
2284:                     existing,
2285:                     actor_user_id,
2286:                     source_plan,
2287:                 )
2288:                 # A prior QR failure is recoverable through the same idempotent
2289:                 # request and must never trigger another WB deliver mutation.
2290:                 await _fetch_supply_qr_after_deliver(session, confirmed_supply, http_client, token)
2291:                 return confirmed_supply
2292:         if existing.state in {
2293:             WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING,
2294:             WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
2295:         }:
2296:             observed_operation_state = existing.state
2297:             token = await _require_marketplace_token(session, tenant_id, supply_read.seller_id)
2298:             reconcile_state = await reconcile_supply_delivered(
2299:                 http_client,
2300:                 api_token=token,
2301:                 wb_supply_id=supply_read.wb_supply_id,
2302:             )
2303:             supply = await _get_supply_for_update(session, tenant_id, supply_id, with_trbxes=True)
2304:             if supply is None:
2305:                 raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
2306:             # Reconcile happened before the supply lock.  Another request may
2307:             # have completed the same durable operation while this request was
2308:             # waiting.  Refresh under the lock so an old observation can never
2309:             # overwrite a definitive FAILED/CONFIRMED state.
2310:             await session.refresh(existing)
2311:             if existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_FAILED:
2312:                 failed_code = existing.error_code or "wb_delivery_failed"
2313:                 await session.rollback()
2314:                 raise FbsShipmentError(
2315:                     failed_code,
2316:                     message=(
2317:                         "Параллельная попытка уже получила окончательный ответ WB. "
2318:                         "Повторите передачу отдельной попыткой."
2319:                     ),
2320:                     context={"operation_state": "failed"},
2321:                     retryable=False,
2322:                     http_status=409,
2323:                 )
2324:             if existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED:
2325:                 reconcile_state = WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED
2326:             if (
2327:                 existing.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING_CONFIRMATION
2328:                 and observed_operation_state != WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING_CONFIRMATION
2329:                 and reconcile_state != WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED
2330:             ):
2331:                 # The readback was made before another request recorded an
2332:                 # ambiguous WB mutation.  Its earlier done=false observation
2333:                 # is stale and cannot authorize a second external call.
2334:                 await session.rollback()
2335:                 raise FbsShipmentError(
2336:                     "wb_pending_confirmation",
2337:                     message="WB пока не подтвердил передачу; слепой повтор запрещён.",
2338:                     context={"operation_state": "pending_confirmation"},
2339:                     retryable=True,
2340:                     http_status=504,
2341:                 )
2342:             if reconcile_state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED:
2343:                 checkpointed = await _load_checkpointed_wb_delivery(session, supply, existing)
2344:                 if checkpointed is None:
2345:                     orders, _, source_plan = await _actual_wb_orders_and_source_plan(
2346:                         session,
2347:                         tenant_id,
2348:                         supply,
2349:                         http_client,
