2350:                         token,
2351:                         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
2352:                     )
2353:                 else:
2354:                     orders, source_plan = checkpointed
2355:                 await _persist_confirmed_delivery(
2356:                     session, supply, orders, existing, actor_user_id, source_plan
2357:                 )
2358:                 await _fetch_supply_qr_after_deliver(session, supply, http_client, token)
2359:                 return supply
2360: 
2361:             if reconcile_state == WB_RECONCILE_NOT_DELIVERED:
2362:                 if existing.idempotency_key == idempotency_key:
2363:                     # WB definitively reports `done=false`; the same durable
2364:                     # attempt can safely continue from its frozen checkpoint.
2365:                     resumable_operation = existing
2366:                 else:
2367:                     # A different client key means an explicit new attempt.
2368:                     # Close the old prepared attempt, then let the new key take
2369:                     # its own current preflight/source snapshot.
2370:                     await mark_operation_failed(
2371:                         session,
2372:                         existing,
2373:                         error_code="wb_not_delivered",
2374:                         wb_supply_id=supply_read.wb_supply_id,
2375:                         local_supply_id=supply_id,
2376:                     )
2377:                     await session.commit()
2378:                     if (
2379:                         existing_by_key is not None
2380:                         and existing_by_key.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_FAILED
2381:                     ):
2382:                         raise FbsShipmentError("idempotency_key_reused", http_status=409)
2383:                     return await deliver_supply(
2384:                         session,
2385:                         tenant_id,
2386:                         supply_id,
2387:                         http_client,
2388:                         idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
2389:                         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
2390:                         confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
2391:                         ozon_provider=ozon_provider,
2392:                     )
2393:             else:
2394:                 await mark_operation_pending_confirmation(
2395:                     session,
2396:                     existing,
2397:                     wb_supply_id=supply_read.wb_supply_id,
2398:                     local_supply_id=supply_id,
2399:                     error_code="wb_pending_confirmation",
2400:                 )
2401:                 await session.commit()
2402:                 raise FbsShipmentError(
2403:                     "wb_pending_confirmation",
2404:                     message="WB пока не подтвердил передачу; слепой повтор запрещён.",
2405:                     context={"operation_state": "pending_confirmation"},
2406:                     retryable=True,
2407:                     http_status=504,
2408:                 )
2409: 
2410:     supply = await _get_supply_for_update(
2411:         session,
2412:         tenant_id,
2413:         supply_id,
2414:         with_trbxes=True,
2415:     )
2416:     if supply is None:
2417:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
2418: 
2419:     # Two tabs can arrive with different client keys before either one creates
2420:     # its journal row.  The supply row serializes that race; re-check under the
2421:     # lock and hand recovery to the already durable operation instead of ever
2422:     # issuing a second WB deliver mutation.
2423:     raced_operation = await get_active_deliver_operation_for_supply(
2424:         session,
2425:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
2426:         seller_id=supply.seller_id,
2427:         local_supply_id=supply.id,
2428:     )
2429:     if raced_operation is not None and (existing is None or raced_operation.id != existing.id):
2430:         await session.rollback()
2431:         return await deliver_supply(
2432:             session,
2433:             tenant_id,
2434:             supply_id,
2435:             http_client,
2436:             idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
2437:             actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
2438:             confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
2439:             ozon_provider=ozon_provider,
2440:         )
2441:     if supply.delivery_type not in _DELIVER_ALLOWED_DELIVERY_TYPES:
2442:         raise FbsShipmentError("wrong_delivery_type")
2443:     if supply.status in _DELIVER_BLOCKED_SUPPLY_STATUSES:
2444:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_bad_status", http_status=409)
2445: 
2446:     operation = resumable_operation
2447:     if operation is None:
2448:         token = await _require_marketplace_token(session, tenant_id, supply.seller_id)
2449:         orders, _, source_plan = await _sync_and_validate_deliver(
2450:             session,
2451:             tenant_id,
2452:             supply,
2453:             http_client,
2454:             token,
2455:             confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
2456:             actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
2457:         )
2458:         from app.services.fbs_cancelled_after_pack_service import exclude_cancelled_delivery_orders
2459: 
2460:         await exclude_cancelled_delivery_orders(session, supply)
2461:         operation = await create_pending_deliver_operation(
2462:             session,
2463:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
2464:             seller_id=supply.seller_id,
2465:             idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
2466:             request_hash=request_hash,
2467:             local_supply_id=supply.id,
2468:             confirmed_preflight_version=confirmed_preflight_version,
2469:             created_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
2470:         )
2471:     else:
2472:         token = await _require_marketplace_token(session, tenant_id, supply.seller_id)
2473:         checkpointed = await _load_checkpointed_wb_delivery(session, supply, operation)
2474:         if checkpointed is None:
2475:             raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_checkpoint_incomplete", http_status=409)
2476:         orders, source_plan = checkpointed
2477: 
2478:     # Сначала сохраняем операцию и точный план источников, затем выполняем
2479:     # необратимый запрос WB. Если процесс умрёт после WB 2xx, повтор найдёт
2480:     # pending-операцию, сверится с WB и закончит локальное списание без второго
2481:     # вызова deliver.
2482:     await _stage_wb_shipment_sources(
2483:         session,
2484:         supply,
2485:         orders,
2486:         source_plan,
2487:         operation,
2488:         actor_user_id,
2489:     )
2490:     await session.commit()
2491: 
2492:     # Commit выше делает checkpoint долговечным, но снимает row lock. Сразу
2493:     # берём поставку под блокировку снова и держим её до результата WB, чтобы
2494:     # параллельная вкладка не выполнила вторую внешнюю передачу.
2495:     locked_supply = await _get_supply_for_update(
2496:         session,
2497:         tenant_id,
2498:         supply_id,
2499:         with_trbxes=True,
2500:     )
2501:     if locked_supply is None:
2502:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
2503:     supply = locked_supply
2504:     # Another request may have completed this operation between our durable
2505:     # checkpoint commit and reacquiring the supply lock.  The supply lock
2506:     # serializes the external mutation; refresh the journal under that lock so
2507:     # a stale in-memory PENDING object can never issue or overwrite a second
2508:     # WB call.
2509:     await session.refresh(operation)
2510:     if operation.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_CONFIRMED:
2511:         checkpointed = await _load_checkpointed_wb_delivery(session, supply, operation)
2512:         if checkpointed is None:
2513:             raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_checkpoint_incomplete", http_status=409)
2514:         orders, source_plan = checkpointed
2515:         await _persist_confirmed_delivery(
2516:             session, supply, orders, operation, actor_user_id, source_plan
2517:         )
2518:         await _fetch_supply_qr_after_deliver(session, supply, http_client, token)
2519:         return supply
2520:     if operation.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_FAILED:
2521:         failed_code = operation.error_code or "wb_delivery_failed"
2522:         await session.rollback()
2523:         raise FbsShipmentError(
2524:             failed_code,
2525:             message="Параллельная попытка уже получила окончательный отказ WB.",
2526:             context={"operation_state": "failed"},
2527:             retryable=False,
2528:             http_status=409,
2529:         )
2530:     if operation.state == WB_OPERATION_STATE_PENDING_CONFIRMATION:
2531:         await session.rollback()
2532:         raise FbsShipmentError(
2533:             "wb_pending_confirmation",
2534:             message="WB пока не подтвердил передачу; слепой повтор запрещён.",
2535:             context={"operation_state": "pending_confirmation"},
2536:             retryable=True,
2537:             http_status=504,
2538:         )
2539:     if supply.status in _DELIVER_BLOCKED_SUPPLY_STATUSES:
2540:         await mark_operation_failed(
2541:             session,
2542:             operation,
2543:             error_code="supply_bad_status",
2544:             wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
2545:             local_supply_id=supply.id,
2546:         )
2547:         await session.commit()
2548:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_bad_status", http_status=409)
2549:     checkpointed = await _load_checkpointed_wb_delivery(session, supply, operation)
2550:     if checkpointed is None:
2551:         raise FbsShipmentError("fbs_shipment_checkpoint_incomplete", http_status=409)
2552:     orders, source_plan = checkpointed
2553: 
2554:     try:
2555:         await deliver_marketplace_supply(
2556:             http_client,
2557:             api_token=token,
2558:             supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
2559:         )
2560:     except WildberriesBusinessError as exc:
2561:         meta_context = _meta_validation_context(exc)
2562:         message, retryable = _meta_validation_message(exc)
2563:         await mark_operation_failed(
2564:             session,
2565:             operation,
2566:             error_code="meta_validation_fail",
2567:             # ⛔ Сырой ответ WB сохраняем обязательно.
2568:             #
2569:             # 02.09.2026 склад шесть раз подряд получил «повторите через минуту»
2570:             # и встал. В базе от этих попыток остался пустой список
2571:             # meta_validation и больше ничего: что именно сказал Wildberries,
2572:             # восстановить было нечем — ни в логах, ни в операции. Разбор
2573:             # занял час и потребовал лезть на боевой сервер.
2574:             error_context={
2575:                 "meta_validation": meta_context,
2576:                 "wb_code": exc.wb_code,
2577:                 "wb_message": exc.message,
2578:                 "wb_response_body": truncate_wb_response_body(exc.response_body),
2579:                 "wb_endpoint": exc.endpoint,
2580:             },
2581:             wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
2582:             local_supply_id=supply.id,
2583:         )
2584:         await session.commit()
2585:         raise FbsShipmentError(
2586:             "meta_validation_fail",
2587:             message=message,
2588:             context={"meta_validation": meta_context},
2589:             retryable=retryable,
2590:             http_status=409,
2591:         ) from exc
2592:     except WildberriesClientError as exc:
2593:         if exc.code == "transport_error":
2594:             ref = wb_error_ref()
2595:             log_wb_client_error(
2596:                 logger,
2597:                 "fbs shipment WB deliver timeout",
2598:                 exc,
2599:                 tenant_id=tenant_id,
2600:                 seller_id=supply.seller_id,
2601:                 local_entity_id=supply.id,
2602:                 wb_object_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
2603:                 ref=ref,
2604:             )
2605:             await mark_operation_pending_confirmation(
2606:                 session,
2607:                 operation,
2608:                 wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
2609:                 local_supply_id=supply.id,
2610:                 error_code="wb_timeout",
2611:             )
2612:             await session.commit()
2613:             raise FbsShipmentError(
2614:                 "wb_timeout",
2615:                 message="WB не подтвердил передачу — повторите операцию.",
2616:                 context={"operation_state": "pending_confirmation"},
2617:                 retryable=True,
2618:                 http_status=504,
2619:             ) from exc
2620:         error = _shipment_error_from_wb(
2621:             exc,
2622:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
2623:             seller_id=supply.seller_id,
2624:             local_entity_id=supply.id,
2625:             wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
2626:             event="fbs shipment WB deliver failed",
2627:             retryable=False,
2628:             http_status=502,
2629:         )
2630:         await mark_operation_failed(
2631:             session,
2632:             operation,
2633:             error_code=error.code,
2634:             error_context=error.context,
2635:             wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
2636:             local_supply_id=supply.id,
2637:         )
2638:         await session.commit()
2639:         raise error from exc
2640: 
2641:     await _persist_confirmed_delivery(
2642:         session, supply, orders, operation, actor_user_id, source_plan
2643:     )
2644:     await _fetch_supply_qr_after_deliver(session, supply, http_client, token)
2645:     return supply

2647: 
2648: async def get_supply_barcode(
2649:     session: AsyncSession,
2650:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
2651:     supply_id: uuid.UUID,
2652:     http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
2653:     *,
2654:     type: str = "png",
2655: ) -> bytes:
2656:     supply = await _get_supply_read(session, tenant_id, supply_id)
2657:     if supply is None:
2658:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
2659:     if supply.status not in {FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_IN_DELIVERY, FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_DONE}:
2660:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_bad_status", http_status=409)
2661: 
2662:     if supply.barcode_file:
2663:         cached = _resolve_barcode_path(supply.barcode_file)
2664:         if cached.is_file():
2665:             return cached.read_bytes()
2666: 
2667:     if supply.marketplace == "ozon":
2668:         client_id, api_key = await _ozon_credentials(
2669:             session,
2670:             tenant_id,
2671:             supply.seller_id,
2672:         )
2673:         provider = build_ozon_provider()
2674:         png_bytes = await _store_ozon_supply_qr(
2675:             session,
2676:             supply,
2677:             provider,
2678:             client_id=client_id,
2679:             api_key=api_key,
2680:         )
2681:         supply.barcode_file = _save_barcode_png(supply.id, png_bytes)
2682:         await session.flush()
2683:         return png_bytes
2684: 
2685:     token = await _require_marketplace_token(session, tenant_id, supply.seller_id)
2686:     try:
2687:         png_bytes = await fetch_marketplace_supply_barcode(
2688:             http_client,
2689:             api_token=token,
2690:             supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
2691:             type=type,
2692:         )
2693:     except WildberriesClientError as exc:
2694:         raise _shipment_error_from_wb(
2695:             exc,
2696:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
2697:             seller_id=supply.seller_id,
2698:             local_entity_id=supply.id,
2699:             wb_supply_id=supply.wb_supply_id,
2700:             event="fbs shipment WB supply barcode fetch failed",
2701:         ) from exc
2702: 
2703:     supply.barcode_file = _save_barcode_png(supply.id, png_bytes)
2704:     await session.flush()
2705:     return png_bytes
2706: 
2707: 
2708: async def retry_supply_qr(
2709:     session: AsyncSession,
2710:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
2711:     supply_id: uuid.UUID,
2712:     http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
2713: ) -> FbsSupply:
2714:     """Safely fetch a missing supply QR after WB delivery was confirmed (any delivery_type)."""
2715:     supply = await _get_supply_for_update(session, tenant_id, supply_id, with_trbxes=True)
2716:     if supply is None:
2717:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_not_found")
2718:     if supply.status not in {FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_IN_DELIVERY, FBS_SUPPLY_STATUS_DONE}:
2719:         raise FbsShipmentError("supply_bad_status", http_status=409)
2720: 
2721:     if supply.marketplace == "ozon":
2722:         client_id, api_key = await _ozon_credentials(
2723:             session,
2724:             tenant_id,
2725:             supply.seller_id,
2726:         )
2727:         await _store_ozon_supply_qr(
2728:             session,
2729:             supply,
2730:             build_ozon_provider(),
2731:             client_id=client_id,
2732:             api_key=api_key,
2733:         )
2734:         return supply
2735: 
2736:     token = await _require_marketplace_token(session, tenant_id, supply.seller_id)
2737:     await _fetch_supply_qr_after_deliver(session, supply, http_client, token)
2738:     return supply
END_SOURCE

79: # Окно полного обхода заданий: столько дней назад просим у WB. Совпадает с
80: # умолчанием WB и с его же ограничением «максимум 30 календарных дней за запрос».
81: ORDERS_SWEEP_WINDOW_DAYS = 30
82: # Размер страницы обхода. WB разрешает до 1000, но каждая страница разбирается
83: # и пишется в одной транзакции: тысяча строк — это долгие блокировки на каждом
84: # из трёх десятков селлеров. Берём середину — охват тот же за счёт числа страниц,
85: # транзакция вдвое короче, а двадцать запросов на селлера укладываются в лимит WB
86: # (300 запросов в минуту на аккаунт).
87: ORDERS_SWEEP_PAGE_LIMIT = 500
88: MAX_SUPPLIES_PAGES = 10
89: 
90: RESERVE_STATUS_WAREHOUSE_REMAP_CONFLICT = "warehouse_remap_conflict"
91: 
92: CANCEL_LIKE_WB_STATUSES = frozenset(
93:     {
94:         "cancel",
95:         "canceled",
96:         "cancelled",
97:         "canceled_by_client",
98:         "canceled_by_carrier",
99:         "declined_by_client",
100:     }
101: )
102: 
103: DEFECT_WB_STATUS = "defect"
104: 
105: NO_RESERVE_WB_STATUSES = CANCEL_LIKE_WB_STATUSES | {DEFECT_WB_STATUS}
106: 
107: TERMINAL_FBS_STATUSES = frozenset(
108:     {FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED, FBS_ORDER_STATUS_DONE, FBS_ORDER_STATUS_DEFECT}
109: )
110: 
111: STATUSES_EXCLUDED_FROM_WB_SYNC = TERMINAL_FBS_STATUSES
112: 
113: SYNC_STATUS_BATCH_SIZE = 500
114: MAX_SYNC_STATUS_BATCHES = 20
115: 
116: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
117: 
118: # Повтор списания пула при занятой базе: рядом может писать фоновая публикация остатка.

121: class WbMarketplaceOrdersError(Exception):
122:     def __init__(
123:         self,
124:         code: str,
125:         *,
126:         message: str | None = None,
127:         context: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
128:         retryable: bool = False,
129:     ) -> None:
130:         self.code = code
131:         self.message = message or code
132:         self.context = context or {}
133:         self.retryable = retryable
134:         super().__init__(code)
135: 
136: 
137: def _parse_wb_datetime(raw: Any) -> datetime:
138:     if isinstance(raw, datetime):
139:         return raw if raw.tzinfo is not None else raw.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
140:     if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
141:         return datetime.now(tz=UTC)
142:     text = raw.strip().replace("Z", "+00:00")
143:     try:
144:         parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(text)
145:     except ValueError:
146:         return datetime.now(tz=UTC)
147:     return parsed if parsed.tzinfo is not None else parsed.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
148: 
149: 
150: def _first_barcode(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
151:     skus = row.get("skus")
152:     if isinstance(skus, list) and skus:
153:         first = skus[0]
154:         if isinstance(first, str) and first.strip():
155:             return first.strip()
156:     for key in ("barcode", "sku"):
157:         val = row.get(key)
158:         if isinstance(val, str) and val.strip():
159:             return val.strip()
160:     return None
161: 
162: 
163: def _status_from_row(row: dict[str, Any], keys: tuple[str, ...]) -> str | None:
164:     for key in keys:
165:         val = row.get(key)
166:         if isinstance(val, str) and val.strip():
167:             return val.strip().lower()
168:     return None
169: 
170: 
171: def _supplier_status_from_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
172:     return _status_from_row(row, ("supplierStatus", "status"))
173: 
174: 
175: def _wb_status_from_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
176:     return _status_from_row(row, ("wbStatus", "status"))
177: 
178: 
179: def _is_cancel_like_wb_status(wb_status: str | None) -> bool:
180:     return wb_status is not None and wb_status.strip().lower() in CANCEL_LIKE_WB_STATUSES
181: 
182: 
183: def _is_cancelled_wb_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
184:     wb_status = _wb_status_from_row(row)
185:     supplier_status = _supplier_status_from_row(row)
186:     return _is_cancel_like_wb_status(wb_status) or _is_cancel_like_wb_status(supplier_status)
187: 
188: 
189: def _local_status_from_wb_statuses(
190:     *,
191:     wb_status: str | None,
192:     supplier_status: str | None,
193: ) -> str:
194:     if _is_cancel_like_wb_status(wb_status) or _is_cancel_like_wb_status(supplier_status):
195:         return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED
196:     if wb_status == "sold":
197:         return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_DONE
198:     if wb_status == "sorted":
199:         return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_SORTED
200:     if wb_status == DEFECT_WB_STATUS:
201:         return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_DEFECT
202:     if supplier_status is not None and supplier_status != FBS_ORDER_STATUS_NEW:
203:         return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_EXTERNAL_PROCESSING
204:     return FBS_ORDER_STATUS_NEW
205: 
206: 
207: def _can_pvz_value_from_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> bool | None:
208:     """Разрешена ли сдача заказа через ПВЗ.
209: 
210:     Настоящее поле WB — `isPickupPointShipmentAllowed`. Раньше читались `canPvz`
211:     и `isPvz`, которых в ответе WB нет вовсе, поэтому признак всегда выходил false
212:     и маршрут ПВЗ был заблокирован для всех заказов. Старые имена оставлены
213:     запасными: их отдаёт эмулятор и старые фикстуры.
214:     """
215:     for key in ("isPickupPointShipmentAllowed", "canPvz", "isPvz"):
216:         value = row.get(key)
217:         if value is not None:
218:             return bool(value)
219:     return None
220: 
221: 
222: def _can_pvz_from_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
223:     value = _can_pvz_value_from_row(row)
224:     if value is not None:
225:         return value
226:     return False
227: 
228: 
229: def _is_legal_order(row: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
230:     if row.get("isLegal") is True:
231:         return True
232:     options = row.get("options")
233:     return isinstance(options, dict) and options.get("isB2B") is True
234: 
235: 
236: def _cargo_type_label(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
237:     raw = row.get("cargoType")
238:     if raw is None:
239:         return None
240:     mapping = {1: "mgt", 2: "kgt", 3: "sgt"}
241:     if isinstance(raw, int):
242:         return mapping.get(raw, str(raw))
243:     if isinstance(raw, str) and raw.strip():
244:         return raw.strip()
245:     return None
246:

248: async def available_qty_for_fbs_reserve(
249:     session: AsyncSession,
250:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
251:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
252:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
253:     *,
254:     exclude_order_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
255: ) -> int:
256:     """FBS direction pool minus active FBS reserves; no FBS direction means 0."""
257:     return await fbs_available_qty_for_product(
258:         session,
259:         tenant_id,
260:         warehouse_id,
261:         product_id,
262:         exclude_fbs_order_id=exclude_order_id,
263:     )

267:     office = row.get("officeId")
268:     if office is None:
269:         return None
270:     return int(office)

273: def _wb_warehouse_id_from_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> int | None:
274:     wh = row.get("warehouseId")
275:     if wh is None:
276:         return None
277:     return int(wh)

279: 
280: async def _resolve_wms_warehouse_from_binding(
281:     session: AsyncSession,
282:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
283:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
284:     wb_warehouse_id: int | None,
285: ) -> uuid.UUID | None:
286:     if wb_warehouse_id is None:
287:         return None
288:     stmt = (
289:         select(FbsWarehouseBinding, Warehouse)
290:         .join(Warehouse, Warehouse.id == FbsWarehouseBinding.wms_warehouse_id)
291:         .where(
292:             FbsWarehouseBinding.tenant_id == tenant_id,
293:             FbsWarehouseBinding.seller_id == seller_id,
294:             # Это вайлдберрисовский путь целиком; с появлением привязок Ozon
295:             # числовой ключ перестал быть уникальным сам по себе.
296:             FbsWarehouseBinding.marketplace == "wb",
297:             FbsWarehouseBinding.wb_warehouse_id == wb_warehouse_id,
298:             FbsWarehouseBinding.is_active.is_(True),
299:             Warehouse.tenant_id == tenant_id,
300:         )
301:     )
302:     res = await session.execute(stmt)
303:     row = res.one_or_none()
304:     if row is None:
305:         binding = await get_or_create_binding_for_sole_operational_warehouse(
306:             session, tenant_id, seller_id, wb_warehouse_id
307:         )
308:         if binding is None or not binding.is_active:
309:             return None
310:         warehouse = await session.get(Warehouse, binding.wms_warehouse_id)
311:         if warehouse is None:
312:             return None
313:     else:
314:         binding, warehouse = row
315:         if is_auto_fbs_wms_warehouse(warehouse):
316:             binding = await get_or_create_binding_for_sole_operational_warehouse(
317:                 session, tenant_id, seller_id, wb_warehouse_id
318:             )
319:             if binding is None or not binding.is_active:
320:                 return None
321:             warehouse = await session.get(Warehouse, binding.wms_warehouse_id)
322:             if warehouse is None:
323:                 return None
324:     if is_auto_fbs_wms_warehouse(warehouse):
325:         binding.stock_sync_enabled = False
326:         await session.flush()
327:         return None
328:     return cast(uuid.UUID, binding.wms_warehouse_id)
329: 
330: 
331: async def _resolve_wms_warehouse_for_wb(
332:     session: AsyncSession,
333:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
334:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
335:     wb_warehouse_id: int | None,
336: ) -> uuid.UUID | None:
337:     return await _resolve_wms_warehouse_from_binding(session, tenant_id, seller_id, wb_warehouse_id)
338: 
339: 
340: async def _get_reservation_warehouse_id(
341:     session: AsyncSession, order_id: uuid.UUID
342: ) -> uuid.UUID | None:
343:     stmt = select(FbsOrderReservation.warehouse_id).where(
344:         FbsOrderReservation.fbs_order_id == order_id
345:     )
346:     res = await session.execute(stmt)
347:     return res.scalar_one_or_none()
348: 
349: 
350: async def _detect_warehouse_remap_conflict(
351:     session: AsyncSession,
352:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
353:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
354:     order: FbsOrder,
355:     new_wb_warehouse_id: int,
356: ) -> bool:
357:     """True when WB warehouse changed but an active reserve pins another WMS warehouse."""
358:     if new_wb_warehouse_id == order.wb_warehouse_id:
359:         return False
360:     reservation_wh = await _get_reservation_warehouse_id(session, order.id)
361:     if reservation_wh is None:
362:         return False
363:     resolved = await _resolve_wms_warehouse_from_binding(
364:         session, tenant_id, seller_id, new_wb_warehouse_id
365:     )
366:     return resolved != reservation_wh
367: 
368: 
369: async def _assign_wms_warehouse_from_binding(
370:     session: AsyncSession,
371:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
372:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
373:     order: FbsOrder,
374:     wb_warehouse_id: int | None,
375: ) -> None:
376:     """Map WB warehouse to local WMS warehouse only through an explicit active binding."""
377:     resolved = await _resolve_wms_warehouse_for_wb(session, tenant_id, seller_id, wb_warehouse_id)
378:     if resolved is None:
379:         if order.warehouse_id is not None:
380:             current_warehouse = await session.get(Warehouse, order.warehouse_id)
381:             if current_warehouse is not None and is_auto_fbs_wms_warehouse(current_warehouse):
382:                 order.reserve_status = RESERVE_STATUS_WAREHOUSE_REMAP_CONFLICT
383:                 return
384:             if current_warehouse is None:
385:                 order.warehouse_id = None
386:         order.reserve_status = RESERVE_STATUS_WAREHOUSE_UNMAPPED
387:         return
388: 
389:     if order.warehouse_id is None:
390:         order.warehouse_id = resolved
391:         return
392: 
393:     if order.warehouse_id == resolved:
394:         return
395: 
396:     reservation_wh = await _get_reservation_warehouse_id(session, order.id)
397:     if reservation_wh is not None and reservation_wh != resolved:
398:         order.reserve_status = RESERVE_STATUS_WAREHOUSE_REMAP_CONFLICT
399:         return
400:     # An already assigned order is warehouse work in progress.  A later
401:     # auto-binding (or a changed external WB route) may make another warehouse
402:     # resolvable, but must not silently transfer the document.
403:     return
404: 
405: 
406: async def _map_product(
407:     session: AsyncSession,
408:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
409:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
410:     *,
411:     wb_barcode: str | None,
412:     wb_nm_id: int | None,
413:     wb_chrt_id: int | None,
414: ) -> Product | None:
415:     async def one_or_none(stmt: Select[tuple[Product]]) -> Product | None:
416:         rows = list((await session.execute(stmt.limit(2))).scalars().all())
417:         if len(rows) == 1:
418:             return rows[0]
419:         return None
420: 
421:     if wb_chrt_id is not None:
422:         stmt = select(Product).where(
423:             Product.tenant_id == tenant_id,
424:             Product.seller_id == seller_id,
425:             Product.wb_chrt_id == wb_chrt_id,
426:         )
427:         product = await one_or_none(stmt)
428:         if product is not None:
429:             return product
430:     if wb_barcode:
431:         stmt = select(Product).where(
432:             Product.tenant_id == tenant_id,
433:             Product.seller_id == seller_id,
434:             Product.wb_barcode == wb_barcode,
435:         )
436:         product = await one_or_none(stmt)
437:         if product is not None:
438:             return product
439:     if wb_nm_id is not None:
440:         stmt = select(Product).where(
441:             Product.tenant_id == tenant_id,
442:             Product.seller_id == seller_id,
443:             Product.wb_nm_id == wb_nm_id,
444:         )
445:         return await one_or_none(stmt)
446:     return None
447: 
448: 
449: async def _get_order_by_wb_id(
450:     session: AsyncSession,
451:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
452:     wb_order_id: int,
453: ) -> FbsOrder | None:
454:     stmt = select(FbsOrder).where(
455:         FbsOrder.seller_id == seller_id,
456:         FbsOrder.wb_order_id == wb_order_id,
457:     )
458:     res = await session.execute(stmt.execution_options(populate_existing=True))
459:     return res.scalar_one_or_none()
460: 
461: 
462: async def _order_has_reservation(session: AsyncSession, order_id: uuid.UUID) -> bool:
463:     legacy = await session.scalar(
464:         select(FbsOrderReservation.id).where(FbsOrderReservation.fbs_order_id == order_id)
465:     )
466:     if legacy is not None:
467:         return True
468:     return (
469:         await session.scalar(
470:             select(FbsOrderProductReservation.id)
471:             .join(
472:                 FbsOrderProduct, FbsOrderProduct.id == FbsOrderProductReservation.order_product_id
473:             )
474:             .where(FbsOrderProduct.order_id == order_id)
475:         )
476:     ) is not None
477: 
478: 
479: async def _try_reserve_order(
480:     session: AsyncSession,
481:     order: FbsOrder,
482: ) -> None:
483:     if order.status in TERMINAL_FBS_STATUSES:
484:         return
485:     if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_EXTERNAL_PROCESSING:
486:         return
487:     if order.wb_status is not None and order.wb_status.lower() in NO_RESERVE_WB_STATUSES:
488:         return
489:     if (
490:         order.supplier_status is not None
491:         and order.supplier_status.strip().lower() != FBS_ORDER_STATUS_NEW
492:     ):
493:         return
494:     from app.services.inventory_service import update_fbs_order_reservation
495: 
496:     await update_fbs_order_reservation(session, order, reserve=True)
497: 
498: 
499: async def _release_reservation(session: AsyncSession, order: FbsOrder) -> None:
500:     from app.services.inventory_service import update_fbs_order_reservation
501: 
502:     await update_fbs_order_reservation(session, order, reserve=False)
503: 
504: 
505: async def _move_new_order_to_external_processing(
506:     session: AsyncSession,
507:     order: FbsOrder,
508: ) -> None:
509:     if order.status != FBS_ORDER_STATUS_NEW or order.supply_id is not None:
510:         return
511:     order.status = FBS_ORDER_STATUS_EXTERNAL_PROCESSING
512:     await _release_reservation(session, order)
513: 
514: 
515: # Internal response annotation: datetime objects cannot arrive in WB JSON.
516: # Capture before pagination/DB locks; never persist this key as marketplace data.
517: _OBSERVED_AT_KEY = "_wms_cancel_observed_at"
518: 
519: 
520: def _observe_rows(rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
521:     at = datetime.now(UTC)
522:     return [{**row, _OBSERVED_AT_KEY: at} for row in rows]
523: 
524: 
525: def _row_observed_at(row: dict[str, Any] | None) -> datetime:
526:     value = row.get(_OBSERVED_AT_KEY) if row else None
527:     return value if isinstance(value, datetime) else datetime.now(UTC)
528: 
529: 
530: async def _apply_wb_row_to_existing(
531:     session: AsyncSession,
532:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
533:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
534:     existing: FbsOrder,
535:     row: dict[str, Any],
536:     *,
537:     preserve_unmapped_warehouse: bool = False,
538: ) -> None:
539:     observed_at = _row_observed_at(row)
540:     wb_barcode = _first_barcode(row)
541:     wb_nm_id = row.get("nmId")
542:     wb_nm_id_int = int(wb_nm_id) if wb_nm_id is not None else None
543: 
544:     existing.wb_rid = row.get("rid") if isinstance(row.get("rid"), str) else existing.wb_rid
545:     existing.wb_nm_id = wb_nm_id_int
546:     existing.wb_chrt_id = (
547:         int(row["chrtId"]) if row.get("chrtId") is not None else existing.wb_chrt_id
548:     )
549:     existing.wb_article = (
550:         str(row["article"]) if row.get("article") is not None else existing.wb_article
551:     )
552:     existing.wb_barcode = wb_barcode or existing.wb_barcode
553:     existing.price = int(row["price"]) if row.get("price") is not None else existing.price
554:     existing.is_legal = _is_legal_order(row)
555:     existing.cargo_type = _cargo_type_label(row)
556:     office_id = _wb_office_id_from_row(row)
557:     if office_id is not None:
558:         existing.wb_office_id = office_id
559:     wb_wh_id = _wb_warehouse_id_from_row(row)
560:     if wb_wh_id is not None:
561:         if await _detect_warehouse_remap_conflict(
562:             session, tenant_id, seller_id, existing, wb_wh_id
563:         ):
564:             existing.reserve_status = RESERVE_STATUS_WAREHOUSE_REMAP_CONFLICT
565:         else:
566:             existing.wb_warehouse_id = wb_wh_id
567:     wb_supply_id = _wb_supply_id_from_order_row(row)
568:     if wb_supply_id is not None:
569:         existing.wb_supply_id = wb_supply_id
570:     can_pvz = _can_pvz_value_from_row(row)
571:     if can_pvz is not None:
572:         existing.can_pvz = can_pvz
573:     supplier_status = _supplier_status_from_row(row)
574:     wb_status = _wb_status_from_row(row)
575:     if supplier_status is not None or wb_status is not None:
576:         await _apply_wb_status_to_order(
577:             session,
578:             existing,
579:             wb_status,
580:             supplier_status=supplier_status,
581:             actor_user_id=None,
582:             row=row,
583:             received_at=observed_at,
584:         )
585:     apply_wb_meta_requirements_to_order(existing, row)
586:     if preserve_unmapped_warehouse:
587:         if existing.warehouse_id is None:
588:             existing.reserve_status = RESERVE_STATUS_WAREHOUSE_UNMAPPED
589:     else:
590:         await _assign_wms_warehouse_from_binding(
591:             session, tenant_id, seller_id, existing, existing.wb_warehouse_id
592:         )
593:     if existing.product_id is None:
594:         product = await _map_product(
595:             session,
596:             tenant_id,
597:             seller_id,
598:             wb_barcode=existing.wb_barcode,
599:             wb_nm_id=existing.wb_nm_id,
600:             wb_chrt_id=existing.wb_chrt_id,
601:         )
602:         if product is not None:
603:             existing.product_id = product.id
604:             existing.mapping_status = MAPPING_STATUS_MAPPED
605:     if existing.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED:
606:         from app.services.fbs_cancel_return_document_service import (
607:             maybe_create_cancel_return_document,
608:         )
609: 
610:         await maybe_create_cancel_return_document(session, existing, row=row)
611:     try:
612:         async with session.begin_nested():
613:             await _try_reserve_order(session, existing)
614:     except IntegrityError:
615:         pass
616: 
617: 
618: async def upsert_order_from_wb_row(
619:     session: AsyncSession,
620:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
621:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
622:     row: dict[str, Any],
623:     *,
624:     preserve_unmapped_warehouse: bool = False,
625: ) -> tuple[FbsOrder, bool]:
626:     """Returns (order, created).
627: 
628:     "shortfall": int} across calls: units actually subtracted from
629:     fbs_binding_stock_pools for this row's order, and units that could not
630:     be subtracted because the pool was already lower than the order needed
631:     (a WB-cabinet desync, not an error to raise on). Callers that don't
632:     care can omit it; the 2-tuple return is unchanged so no call site breaks.
633:     """
634:     row = {**row, _OBSERVED_AT_KEY: _row_observed_at(row)}
635:     wb_order_id_raw = row.get("id")
636:     if wb_order_id_raw is None:
637:         raise WbMarketplaceOrdersError("missing_wb_order_id")
638:     wb_order_id = int(wb_order_id_raw)
639:     created_at_wb = _parse_wb_datetime(row.get("createdAt"))
640:     deadline_at = created_at_wb + timedelta(hours=FBS_DEADLINE_HOURS)
641:     wb_barcode = _first_barcode(row)
642:     wb_nm_id = row.get("nmId")
643:     wb_nm_id_int = int(wb_nm_id) if wb_nm_id is not None else None
644:     wb_chrt_id = row.get("chrtId")
645:     wb_chrt_id_int = int(wb_chrt_id) if wb_chrt_id is not None else None
646: 
647:     existing = await _get_order_by_wb_id(session, seller_id, wb_order_id)
648:     if existing is not None:
649:         await _apply_wb_row_to_existing(
650:             session,
651:             tenant_id,
652:             seller_id,
653:             existing,
654:             row,
655:             preserve_unmapped_warehouse=preserve_unmapped_warehouse,
656:         )
657:         return existing, False
658: 
659:     product = await _map_product(
660:         session,
661:         tenant_id,
662:         seller_id,
663:         wb_barcode=wb_barcode,
664:         wb_nm_id=wb_nm_id_int,
665:         wb_chrt_id=wb_chrt_id_int,
666:     )
667:     mapping_status = MAPPING_STATUS_MAPPED if product is not None else MAPPING_STATUS_MISSING
668:     wb_warehouse_id = _wb_warehouse_id_from_row(row)
669:     wms_warehouse_id = None
670:     if not preserve_unmapped_warehouse:
671:         wms_warehouse_id = await _resolve_wms_warehouse_for_wb(
672:             session, tenant_id, seller_id, wb_warehouse_id
673:         )
674:     if product is None:
675:         reserve_status = RESERVE_STATUS_SKIPPED_NO_PRODUCT
676:     elif wms_warehouse_id is None:
677:         reserve_status = RESERVE_STATUS_WAREHOUSE_UNMAPPED
678:     else:
679:         reserve_status = RESERVE_STATUS_NO_STOCK
680: 
681:     wb_status = _wb_status_from_row(row)
682:     supplier_status = _supplier_status_from_row(row)
683:     order = FbsOrder(
684:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
685:         seller_id=seller_id,
686:         warehouse_id=wms_warehouse_id,
687:         product_id=product.id if product is not None else None,
688:         wb_order_id=wb_order_id,
689:         wb_rid=row.get("rid") if isinstance(row.get("rid"), str) else None,
690:         wb_nm_id=wb_nm_id_int,
691:         wb_chrt_id=wb_chrt_id_int,
692:         wb_article=str(row["article"]) if row.get("article") is not None else None,
693:         wb_barcode=wb_barcode,
694:         price=int(row["price"]) if row.get("price") is not None else None,
695:         is_legal=_is_legal_order(row),
696:         cargo_type=_cargo_type_label(row),
697:         wb_office_id=_wb_office_id_from_row(row),
698:         wb_warehouse_id=wb_warehouse_id,
699:         wb_supply_id=_wb_supply_id_from_order_row(row),
700:         can_pvz=_can_pvz_from_row(row),
701:         status=_local_status_from_wb_statuses(
702:             wb_status=wb_status,
703:             supplier_status=supplier_status,
704:         ),
705:         wb_status=wb_status,
706:         supplier_status=supplier_status,
707:         created_at_wb=created_at_wb,
708:         deadline_at=deadline_at,
709:         mapping_status=mapping_status,
710:         reserve_status=reserve_status,
711:     )
712:     apply_wb_meta_requirements_to_order(order, row)
713:     try:
714:         async with session.begin_nested():
715:             session.add(order)
716:             await session.flush()
717:             await _try_reserve_order(session, order)
718:     except IntegrityError:
719:         raced = await _get_order_by_wb_id(session, seller_id, wb_order_id)
720:         if raced is None:
721:             raise WbMarketplaceOrdersError("duplicate_order_race") from None
722:         await _apply_wb_row_to_existing(
723:             session,
724:             tenant_id,
725:             seller_id,
726:             raced,
727:             row,
728:             preserve_unmapped_warehouse=preserve_unmapped_warehouse,
729:         )
730:         return raced, False
731:     if order.status == FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED:
732:         await _apply_wb_status_to_order(
733:             session, order, wb_status, supplier_status=supplier_status,
734:             actor_user_id=None, row=row, received_at=_row_observed_at(row),
735:         )
736:     return order, True
737: 
738: 
739: async def _apply_wb_status_to_order(
740:     session: AsyncSession,
741:     order: FbsOrder,
742:     wb_status: str | None,
743:     *,
744:     supplier_status: str | None = None,
745:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
746:     row: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
747:     received_at: datetime | None = None,
748: ) -> None:
749:     """Apply status and retain the first WMS observation before side effects."""
750:     observed_at = received_at or _row_observed_at(row)
751:     normalized_wb = (
752:         wb_status.strip().lower() if isinstance(wb_status, str) and wb_status.strip() else None
753:     )
754:     normalized_supplier = (
755:         supplier_status.strip().lower()
756:         if isinstance(supplier_status, str) and supplier_status.strip()
757:         else None
758:     )
759:     if normalized_wb is not None:
760:         order.wb_status = normalized_wb
761:     if normalized_supplier is not None:
762:         order.supplier_status = normalized_supplier
763: 
764:     effective_statuses = tuple(
765:         status for status in (normalized_wb, normalized_supplier) if status is not None
766:     )
767: 
768:     if any(_is_cancel_like_wb_status(status) for status in effective_statuses):
769:         from app.services.fbs_cancellation_service import (
770:             reverse_fbs_order_billing,
771:             reverse_fbs_shipment_if_needed,
772:         )
773:         from app.services.fbs_packaging_integration_service import (
774:             detach_cancelled_order_from_supply,
775:         )
776: 
777:         order.status = FBS_ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED
778:         from app.services.fbs_cancel_return_document_service import (
779:             maybe_create_cancel_return_document,
780:         )
781: 
782:         await maybe_create_cancel_return_document(
783:             session, order, row=row, received_at=observed_at,
784:         )
785:         await reverse_fbs_shipment_if_needed(
786:             session,
787:             order,
788:             actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
789:         )
790:         # WB отменил уже подтверждённый заказ: начисление за него сняли, а
791:         # работы не было. Сторно идёт ровно сюда — руками такой заказ отменить
792:         # нельзя, статусы sorted и done в отмену не пускают.
793:         await reverse_fbs_order_billing(session, order, performer_id=actor_user_id)
794:         await detach_cancelled_order_from_supply(
795:             session,
796:             order.tenant_id,
797:             order,
798:             actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
799:         )
800:         await _release_reservation(session, order)
801:         return
802:     if normalized_wb == "sold":
803:         order.status = FBS_ORDER_STATUS_DONE
804:         # Склад здесь не трогаем ничем. Остаток снимает только наша передача
805:         # поставки маркетплейсу — правило владельца OWN-20.
806:         # Выкуплен: резерв больше не нужен (иначе available навсегда занижен).
807:         await _release_reservation(session, order)
808:         await _charge_confirmed_order(session, order)
809:         return
810:     if normalized_wb == "sorted":
811:         order.status = FBS_ORDER_STATUS_SORTED
812:         await _charge_confirmed_order(session, order)
813:         return
814:     if normalized_wb == DEFECT_WB_STATUS:
815:         order.status = FBS_ORDER_STATUS_DEFECT
816:         await _release_reservation(session, order)
817:         return
818:     if normalized_supplier is not None and normalized_supplier != FBS_ORDER_STATUS_NEW:
819:         await _move_new_order_to_external_processing(session, order)
820:         return
821:     if normalized_wb == "waiting":
822:         if order.status != FBS_ORDER_STATUS_IN_DELIVERY:
823:             return
824:         return
825: 
826: 
827: async def _charge_confirmed_order(session: AsyncSession, order: FbsOrder) -> None:
828:     """Начислить за собранный заказ, когда WB подтвердил, что забрал его.
829: 
830:     Импорт внутри функции: модуль тарификации ходит в биллинг, а биллинг знает
831:     про заказы — на уровне модулей это дало бы цикл.
832:     """
833:     from app.services.fbs_order_billing_service import record_fbs_order_confirmed
834: 
835:     try:
836:         # Точка сохранения обязательна. Голого except мало: ошибка на стороне
837:         # базы (нарушенное ограничение, отвалившееся соединение) переводит всю
838:         # транзакцию PostgreSQL в аварийное состояние, и следующий commit падает
839:         # уже сам по себе. Тогда вместе с начислением откатились бы и статус
840:         # заказа, и списание товара по «sold», и снятие резерва: WB заказ забрал,
841:         # а на складе он остался бы висеть в старой вкладке и искажать остаток.
842:         # Savepoint откатывает только начисление, а внешняя транзакция остаётся
843:         # живой и довозит статусы по всему батчу.
844:         async with session.begin_nested():
845:             await record_fbs_order_confirmed(session, order)
846:     except Exception:
847:         # Деньги важны, но статусы важнее: если начисление почему-то не легло,
848:         # проход опроса обязан довезти статусы по всему батчу, а не откатиться
849:         # вместе с блокировками. Разбираемся по логу, а не по остановленному складу.
850:         logger.exception("fbs order charge skipped: order_id=%s", order.id)
851: 
852: 
853: def _wb_orders_error_from_client(
854:     exc: WildberriesClientError,
855:     *,
856:     ref: str,
857:     extra_context: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
858: ) -> WbMarketplaceOrdersError:
859:     suffix = f"_{exc.status_code}" if exc.status_code else ""
860:     return WbMarketplaceOrdersError(
861:         f"wb_{exc.code}{suffix}",
862:         message=wb_operator_message(exc),
863:         context=wb_error_context(exc, ref=ref, extra=extra_context),
864:         retryable=exc.code == "transport_error",
865:     )
866: 
867: 
868: async def _fetch_status_rows_resilient(
869:     http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
870:     *,
871:     api_token: str,
872:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
873:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
874:     orders_by_wb_id: dict[int, list[FbsOrder]],
875: ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
876:     status_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
877:     wb_ids = sorted(orders_by_wb_id)
878:     for batch in split_marketplace_order_id_batches(wb_ids):
879:         try:
880:             status_rows.extend(
881:                 _observe_rows(await fetch_marketplace_orders_status(
882:                     http_client,
883:                     api_token=api_token,
884:                     order_ids=batch,
885:                 ))
886:             )
887:             continue
888:         except WildberriesClientError as exc:
889:             ref = wb_error_ref()
890:             log_wb_client_error(
891:                 logger,
892:                 "fbs order status WB batch failed; retrying per order",
893:                 exc,
894:                 tenant_id=tenant_id,
895:                 seller_id=seller_id,
896:                 local_entity_id=None,
897:                 wb_object_id=",".join(str(item) for item in batch[:10]),
898:                 ref=ref,
899:                 level=logging.WARNING,
900:             )
901:             if len(batch) == 1:
902:                 continue
903: 
904:         for wb_order_id in batch:
905:             try:
906:                 status_rows.extend(
907:                     _observe_rows(await fetch_marketplace_orders_status(
908:                         http_client,
909:                         api_token=api_token,
910:                         order_ids=[wb_order_id],
911:                     ))
912:                 )
913:             except WildberriesClientError as exc:
914:                 ref = wb_error_ref()
915:                 local_ids = [str(order.id) for order in orders_by_wb_id.get(wb_order_id, [])]
916:                 log_wb_client_error(
917:                     logger,
918:                     "fbs order status WB single order skipped",
919:                     exc,
920:                     tenant_id=tenant_id,
921:                     seller_id=seller_id,
922:                     local_entity_id=",".join(local_ids) or None,
923:                     wb_object_id=wb_order_id,
924:                     ref=ref,
925:                     level=logging.WARNING,
926:                 )
927:                 continue
928:     return status_rows
929: 
930: 
931: async def sync_order_statuses(
932:     session: AsyncSession,
933:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
934:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
935:     http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
936:     api_token: str,
937:     *,
938:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
939: ) -> int:
940:     updated = 0
941:     snapshots: dict[uuid.UUID, dict[str, Any]] = {}
942:     last_created_at: datetime | None = None
943:     last_id: uuid.UUID | None = None
944:     for _batch in range(MAX_SYNC_STATUS_BATCHES):
945:         filters = [
946:             FbsOrder.tenant_id == tenant_id,
947:             FbsOrder.seller_id == seller_id,
948:             FbsOrder.status.not_in(tuple(STATUSES_EXCLUDED_FROM_WB_SYNC)),
949:         ]
950:         if last_created_at is not None and last_id is not None:
951:             filters.append(
952:                 or_(
953:                     FbsOrder.created_at_wb > last_created_at,
954:                     and_(
955:                         FbsOrder.created_at_wb == last_created_at,
956:                         FbsOrder.id > last_id,
957:                     ),
958:                 )
959:             )
960:         stmt = (
961:             select(FbsOrder)
962:             .where(*filters)
963:             .order_by(FbsOrder.created_at_wb.asc(), FbsOrder.id.asc())
964:             .limit(SYNC_STATUS_BATCH_SIZE)
965:         )
966:         res = await session.execute(stmt)
967:         orders = list(res.scalars().all())
968:         if not orders:
969:             break
970: 
971:         orders_by_wb_id: dict[int, list[FbsOrder]] = {}
972:         for order in orders:
973:             if order.wb_order_id <= 0:
974:                 logger.warning(
975:                     "fbs order status skipped invalid local wb_order_id tenant_id=%s "
976:                     "seller_id=%s order_id=%s wb_order_id=%s",
977:                     tenant_id,
978:                     seller_id,
979:                     order.id,
980:                     order.wb_order_id,
981:                 )
982:                 continue
983:             orders_by_wb_id.setdefault(int(order.wb_order_id), []).append(order)
984:         if not orders_by_wb_id:
985:             last_row = orders[-1]
986:             last_created_at = last_row.created_at_wb
987:             last_id = last_row.id
988:             continue
989:         status_rows = await _fetch_status_rows_resilient(
990:             http_client,
991:             api_token=api_token,
992:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
993:             seller_id=seller_id,
994:             orders_by_wb_id=orders_by_wb_id,
995:         )
996: 
997:         by_id: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
998:         for row in status_rows:
999:             oid = row.get("id")
1000:             if oid is not None:
1001:                 by_id[int(oid)] = row
1002: 
1003:         for order in orders:
1004:             status_row = by_id.get(order.wb_order_id)
1005:             if status_row is not None:
1006:                 snapshots[order.id] = status_row
1007: 
1008:         last_row = orders[-1]
1009:         last_created_at = last_row.created_at_wb
1010:         last_id = last_row.id
1011:         if len(orders) < SYNC_STATUS_BATCH_SIZE:
1012:             break
1013:     # Fetch every page before taking write locks. The caller owns the commit;
1014:     # all parents must therefore be locked before the first order, across pages.
1015:     from app.services.fbs_packaging_integration_service import lock_order_batch_packaging_rows
1016: 
1017:     order_ids = list(snapshots)
1018:     await lock_order_batch_packaging_rows(session, tenant_id, order_ids)
1019:     orders = list((await session.scalars(select(FbsOrder).where(
1020:         FbsOrder.tenant_id == tenant_id, FbsOrder.seller_id == seller_id,
1021:         FbsOrder.id.in_(order_ids),
1022:         FbsOrder.status.not_in(tuple(STATUSES_EXCLUDED_FROM_WB_SYNC)),
1023:     ).order_by(FbsOrder.id).execution_options(populate_existing=True))).all())
1024:     for order in orders:
1025:         status_row = snapshots[order.id]
1026:         wb_status = _wb_status_from_row(status_row)
1027:         supplier_status = _supplier_status_from_row(status_row)
1028:         if wb_status is None and supplier_status is None:
1029:             continue
1030:         await _apply_wb_status_to_order(
1031:             session, order, wb_status, supplier_status=supplier_status,
1032:             actor_user_id=actor_user_id, row=status_row,
1033:         )
1034:         updated += 1
1035:     from app.services.fbs_cancel_return_document_service import retry_pending_cancel_returns
1036: 
1037:     await retry_pending_cancel_returns(session, tenant_id, seller_id)
1038:     return updated
1039: 
1040: 
1041: def _wb_supply_id_from_order_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
1042:     for key in ("supplyId", "supplyID", "supply_id", "wb_supply_id"):
1043:         value = row.get(key)
1044:         if value is None:
1045:             continue
1046:         text = str(value).strip()
1047:         if text:
1048:             return text
1049:     return None
1050: 
1051: 
1052: def _wb_supply_name_from_row(row: dict[str, Any], wb_supply_id: str) -> str:
1053:     for key in ("name", "supplyName", "supply_name"):
1054:         value = row.get(key)
1055:         if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
1056:             return value.strip()
1057:     return f"WB supply {wb_supply_id}"
1058: 
1059: 
1060: def _wb_supply_created_at_from_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> datetime | None:
1061:     for key in ("supplyCreatedAt", "supplyCreateDate", "supply_created_at"):
1062:         value = row.get(key)
1063:         if value is not None:
1064:             return _parse_wb_datetime(value)
1065:     return None
1066: 
1067: 
1068: async def _load_unlinked_confirmed_orders(
1069:     session: AsyncSession,
1070:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1071:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
1072: ) -> list[FbsOrder]:
1073:     stmt = (
1074:         select(FbsOrder)
1075:         .where(
1076:             FbsOrder.tenant_id == tenant_id,
1077:             FbsOrder.seller_id == seller_id,
1078:             FbsOrder.supply_id.is_(None),
1079:             FbsOrder.wb_supply_id.is_not(None),
1080:             FbsOrder.supplier_status == "confirm",
1081:             FbsOrder.status.not_in(tuple(TERMINAL_FBS_STATUSES)),
1082:         )
1083:         .order_by(FbsOrder.created_at_wb.asc(), FbsOrder.id.asc())
1084:     )
1085:     res = await session.execute(stmt)
1086:     return list(res.scalars().all())
